This needs a human decision. |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.lane-needs -->", "parent_ids": ["US-001-01", "US-001-02", "US-001-03", "US-001-04", "US-001-05", "US-001-06", "US-002-01", "US-002-02", "US-002-03", "US-002-04", "US-002-05", "US-002-06", "US-003-01", "US-003-02", "US-003-03", "US-003-04", "US-003-05", "US-003-06", "US-004-01", "US-004-02", "US-004-03", "US-004-04", "US-004-05", "US-004-06", "US-005-01", "US-005-02", "US-005-03", "US-005-04", "US-005-05", "US-005-06", "US-006-01", "US-006-02", "US-006-03", "US-006-04", "US-006-05", "US-006-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/auth-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/auth-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: auth\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [Q-BE-003, Q-BE-005, Q-BE-012, CON-BE-003, CON-BE-006]\nscope: [API-AUTH-707260, API-AUTH-692907]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.purpose | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** `auth` provides email one-time-code sign-in. It is an additive sign-in method and existing login methods are unchanged. [requirements.md NFR-016; feedback.md CR-130] It owns:\n\n1. Issuing a 6-digit numeric code for an email address and handing it to the email transport (`API-AUTH-707260`). [BR-029; US-006-06]\n2. Limiting code requests to 3 within a trailing 10 minutes per normalised email. Every request counts, including the first (`CON-BE-011`, reading used). Further requests return 415 and create no code and send no email. [BR-021, BR-028, BR-032; US-004-03]\n3. Verifying a code (`API-AUTH-692907`). A code is valid for 10 minutes from issue and is single-use. [BR-020, BR-023, BR-024]\n4. Counting wrong codes and locking the address for 15 minutes after 5. Verification is refused during the lock. Requesting a code is not refused during the lock, but it stays subject to the resend limit. [BR-022, BR-025, BR-027, BR-032]\n5. Refusing locked-account, wrong, expired and already-used codes with 415, distinguished only by the error code in `backend.error-model`. [BR-033]\n6. Creating the User on first successful verification (`ASM-BE-025`).\n7. Issuing a credential on successful verification. Its form is `Q-BE-012`; its lifetime is `GAP-BE-302`.\n8. Owning the data of USER, OTP_CHALLENGE, SEND_ATTEMPT and LOGIN_LOCK, and the retention deletion of challenges, send attempts and locks. [backend.module-map; NFR-019]\n9. Supplying the login-response content that the published contract must document. [US-004-03]\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- The existing authentication method (`GAP-BE-085`), and the credential checks that `notes` and `search` apply on protected routes.\n- Note ownership and the note access rule (`CON-BE-003`), and retention of notes (not deleted by retention, NFR-019).\n- Email delivery. This module hands the message to the transport (`ASM-BE-027`). The transport region is `Q-BE-005` and `CON-BE-006`.\n- Sign-in screens and their messages, which belong to the web-app surface. [US-006-06]\n- The global error envelope and error-code registry (`backend.error-model`), the published OpenAPI document (`api-docs`), and out-of-scope routing (`scope-guardrail`).\n- Retention of User rows (`GAP-BE-600`).\n\n**Operations owned.** `API-AUTH-707260` and `API-AUTH-692907`, referenced by id only.\n\n**Inbound handoffs.** Three web-app handoffs were listed for this run (HANDOFF-WEBAPP-163212, HANDOFF-WEBAPP-200601, HANDOFF-WEBAPP-743710). Their content was not supplied, so none could be read or answered here (`GAP-BE-602`). The distinct-error-code requirement (BE-AUTH-FR-009) and the open credential question (`Q-BE-012`) are stated for the web-app surface.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\nNo input states column types, nullability or constraints. The platform is PostgreSQL 16 [platform.md]. The types below are a proposal (`ASM-BE-401`). Entity and column names follow `backend.domain-model`. No ORM or migration tool is named (`GAP-BE-089`).\n\n**users**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `user_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | primary key |\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | none | unique; normalised form (`ASM-BE-015`, `GAP-BE-031`) |\n| `created_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | set at first successful verification only (`INV-BE-003`) |\n\nPII: `email` (`ASM-BE-019`). Whether this table is shared with the existing authentication is `GAP-BE-620`.\n\n**otp_challenges**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `challenge_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | primary key |\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | none | normalised; no foreign key to `users`, because the user may not exist yet (`ASM-BE-025`) |\n| `code_digest` | text | NOT NULL | none | storage form undecided (`GAP-BE-033`); the issued code is 6 numeric digits [BR-029] |\n| `issued_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `expires_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | equals `issued_at` + 10 minutes; `CHECK (expires_at = issued_at + interval '10 minutes')` |\n| `consumed_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | set on success; `CHECK (consumed_at IS NULL OR consumed_at >= issued_at)` |\n\nIndexes:\n- btree `(email, issued_at DESC)` serves the lookup of the latest challenge at verification.\n- btree `(issued_at)` serves the retention sweep.\n\n**send_attempts**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `send_attempt_id` | bigint identity | NOT NULL | generated | primary key |\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | none | normalised |\n| `requested_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n\nIndex: btree `(email, requested_at)` serves the count of attempts in the trailing 10 minutes. Only accepted requests write a row (`ASM-BE-403`).\n\n**login_locks**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `email` | text | NOT NULL | none | primary key; normalised |\n| `failed_count` | integer | NOT NULL | 0 | `CHECK (failed_count BETWEEN 0 AND 4)` (`ASM-BE-404`) |\n| `locked_until` | timestamptz | NULL | none | set only at lock entry; null means never locked |\n| `updated_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | last change; retention basis (`ASM-BE-407`) |\n\nThe row for an email is created on first use by either operation. It serves as the per-email serialisation point (BE-AUTH-FR-014).\n\n**Referential integrity.** There are no foreign keys, so there is no `ON DELETE` behaviour. Tables are linked by normalised email value.\n\n**Time.** Every instant is `timestamptz`; all comparisons use the database transaction clock.\n\n**Encryption and PII.** Email addresses and `code_digest` are personal or sensitive data. No input states encryption at rest (`GAP-BE-033`, `GAP-BE-304`), so none is claimed.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\nAll state is per normalised email address (`ASM-BE-015`, `ASM-BE-029`).\n\n### 3.1 OTP challenge\n\nStates: `ISSUED`, `CONSUMED` (terminal), `EXPIRED` (terminal, derived by time: now is strictly after `expires_at`, `ASM-BE-402`, boundary `GAP-BE-306`).\n\nA `SUPERSEDED` state is not defined, because whether a newer code invalidates an older one is `GAP-BE-083`. The working default is `ASM-BE-405`: only the latest challenge for the email is checked.\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `ISSUED` | send accepted | fewer than 3 accepted send attempts in the trailing 10 minutes | challenge row created; send attempt row written; code handed to the transport |\n| `ISSUED` | `CONSUMED` | correct code | email not locked; challenge is the latest; not expired | `consumed_at` set; user created if absent; `failed_count` set to 0; credential issued |\n| `ISSUED` | `ISSUED` | wrong code | email not locked; challenge is the latest and not expired | `failed_count` incremented (`ASM-BE-406`) |\n| `ISSUED` | `EXPIRED` | time passes | now after `expires_at` | none |\n| `CONSUMED` | none | any verify | | refused as already used (`INV-BE-002`); no counter change |\n| `EXPIRED` | none | any verify | | refused as expired; no counter change (`ASM-BE-406`) |\n\n### 3.2 Lock (per email)\n\nStates: `UNLOCKED` (`locked_until` null or not in the future) and `LOCKED` (`locked_until` in the future). Neither is terminal.\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `UNLOCKED` | `LOCKED` | the 5th wrong code | `failed_count` would reach 5 (`ASM-BE-305`) | `locked_until` = now + 15 minutes; `failed_count` reset to 0 (`ASM-BE-404`) |\n| `LOCKED` | `UNLOCKED` | time passes | now at or after `locked_until` | none (derived, no write) |\n| `LOCKED` | `LOCKED` | verify, any code | | refused; `failed_count`, `locked_until` and the challenge unchanged (`ASM-BE-408`, `INV-BE-010`) |\n| `LOCKED` | `LOCKED` | send | send limit not reached | accepted; new challenge issued [BR-032] |\n\n### 3.3 Resend limiter\n\nA count, not a state. A send is accepted when fewer than 3 accepted `send_attempts` fall in the trailing 10 minutes (sliding window, `ASM-BE-044`). A refused send writes no row (`ASM-BE-403`).\n\n### 3.4 Verify decision order\n\nProposed order when several refusals could apply (`GAP-BE-601`, working default `ASM-BE-409`):\n\n1. email `LOCKED` \u2192 locked-account refusal\n2. no challenge for the email \u2192 invalid-code refusal, not counted\n3. latest challenge consumed \u2192 already-used refusal\n4. latest challenge expired \u2192 expired refusal\n5. code differs \u2192 invalid-code refusal, counted\n6. code matches \u2192 success\n\nRefusals use the error codes in `backend.error-model` (OTP_ACCOUNT_LOCKED, OTP_CODE_USED, OTP_CODE_EXPIRED, OTP_CODE_INVALID), all with status 415 [BR-033].\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-001 Issue a code\n**Given** an email address and fewer than 3 accepted send attempts for its normalised form in the trailing 10 minutes,\n**When** `API-AUTH-707260` is called,\n**Then** a 6-digit numeric code is generated, a challenge with `expires_at` = `issued_at` + 10 minutes is stored, a send attempt row is written, the code is handed to the email transport, and success is returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-006-06; BR-020, BR-024, BR-029.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-send-issues-code` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-707260`.\n- **Open.** Transport region `Q-BE-005` / `CON-BE-006`. Input validation `GAP-BE-035`. Code source `GAP-BE-617`. Transport failure `GAP-BE-618`. Success status and body `GAP-BE-604`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-002 Enforce the resend limit\n**Given** 3 accepted send attempts for the email in the trailing 10 minutes,\n**When** `API-AUTH-707260` is called,\n**Then** the request is refused with status 415 and error code OTP_RATE_LIMITED, no challenge is created, no email is sent, and no send attempt row is written.\n- **Traces to.** BR-021, BR-028, BR-032; US-004-03; US-006-06.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-send-limit-415` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-707260`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-003 Send is allowed during a lock\n**Given** the email is `LOCKED`,\n**When** `API-AUTH-707260` is called and the resend limit is not reached,\n**Then** a code is issued as in BE-AUTH-FR-001.\n- **Traces to.** BR-032.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-send-while-locked` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-707260`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-004 Verify a correct code\n**Given** the email is not locked, and the latest challenge for it is unconsumed, not expired, and matches the submitted code,\n**When** `API-AUTH-692907` is called,\n**Then** `consumed_at` is set, a user row is created if none exists, `failed_count` is set to 0, a credential is issued, and success is returned.\n- **Traces to.** BR-023; US-006-06; `ASM-BE-025`; `ASM-BE-305`.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-verify-success` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-692907`.\n- **Open.** The credential is `Q-BE-012` (blocking); its lifetime `GAP-BE-302`. Who may verify is `Q-BE-003` (blocking). Success status and body `GAP-BE-604`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-005 Reject an expired code\n**Given** a challenge issued at T,\n**When** `API-AUTH-692907` is called at an instant after T + 10 minutes,\n**Then** verification is refused with status 415 and error code OTP_CODE_EXPIRED, no credential is issued, and `failed_count` is unchanged.\n- **Boundary.** The instant exactly T + 10 minutes follows `ASM-BE-402` and `GAP-BE-306`.\n- **Traces to.** BR-020, BR-024, BR-026, BR-033; US-006-06.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-verify-expired`, `test-auth-verify-expiry-boundary` (planned, injectable clock).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-692907`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-006 Single use\n**Given** a challenge already consumed,\n**When** `API-AUTH-692907` is called with its code,\n**Then** verification is refused with status 415 and error code OTP_CODE_USED, and no credential is issued.\n- **Concurrency.** Two simultaneous correct submissions of one code result in exactly one success (`INV-BE-011`).\n- **Traces to.** BR-023, BR-033.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-verify-single-use`, `test-auth-verify-concurrent-single-use` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-692907`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-007 Count a wrong code\n**Given** the email is not locked, the latest challenge is unexpired and unconsumed, and the submitted code is wrong,\n**When** `API-AUTH-692907` is called,\n**Then** verification is refused with status 415 and error code OTP_CODE_INVALID, and `failed_count` is incremented by 1.\n- **Traces to.** BR-022, BR-025, BR-027, BR-033; `ASM-BE-305`, `ASM-BE-406`.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-verify-wrong-counts` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-692907`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-008 Lock after 5 wrong codes\n**Given** `failed_count` is 4 and the email is not locked,\n**When** a 5th wrong code is submitted,\n**Then** the submission is refused as wrong, `locked_until` is set to now + 15 minutes, and `failed_count` is reset to 0.\n- **Traces to.** BR-022, BR-025, BR-027; US-006-06.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-lock-on-fifth-wrong` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-692907`.\n- **Open.** Whether the 5th refusal itself reports OTP_CODE_INVALID or OTP_ACCOUNT_LOCKED is `GAP-BE-623`. Default: OTP_CODE_INVALID, because the lock begins after the 5th wrong code [BR-022].\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-009 Refuse verification during a lock\n**Given** the email is `LOCKED`,\n**When** `API-AUTH-692907` is called with a correct or a wrong code,\n**Then** verification is refused with status 415 and error code OTP_ACCOUNT_LOCKED, `failed_count` and `locked_until` are unchanged, and the code is not consumed.\n- **Traces to.** BR-022, BR-027, BR-032, BR-033; US-004-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-verify-locked-correct-code` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-692907`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-010 Distinct error codes on a shared status\n**Given** that the five login refusals share status 415,\n**When** any refusal is returned,\n**Then** the error code identifies the cause, one code per cause, as registered in `backend.error-model`. Consumers must switch on the error code, not the status.\n- **Traces to.** BR-028, BR-033; US-004-03; US-006-06.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-contract-error-codes-distinct` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-707260`, `API-AUTH-692907`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-011 Lock expiry\n**Given** `locked_until` is not in the future,\n**When** `API-AUTH-692907` is called,\n**Then** the email is treated as `UNLOCKED`, and no write is needed to lift the lock.\n- **Traces to.** BR-022, BR-025.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-lock-expires` (planned, injectable clock).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-692907`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-012 No user disclosure\n**Given** any email address,\n**When** either operation is called,\n**Then** no response distinguishes an address that has a User from one that has none (`ASM-BE-400`).\n- **Traces to.** Derived: the abuse controls of BR-021 and BR-022 would otherwise leak account existence. Not stated in any input.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-no-user-disclosure` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-707260`, `API-AUTH-692907`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-013 Log redaction\n**Given** any login event,\n**When** it is logged,\n**Then** the entry contains no unredacted email address and no code.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-018 (title and target metric; the rule text is corrupted, method `GAP-BE-104`).\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-log-redaction` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-707260`, `API-AUTH-692907`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-014 Per-email serialisation\n**Given** concurrent requests for the same normalised email,\n**When** they are processed,\n**Then** the send count, the wrong-code counter, the lock transition and the consumption of a code are each decided under a row-level lock on that email's `login_locks` row, so that no more than 3 sends are accepted per window, the lock is entered exactly once at the 5th wrong code, and a code is consumed at most once.\n- **Traces to.** BR-021, BR-022, BR-023, BR-028 (limits must hold under concurrency).\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-concurrent-send-limit`, `test-auth-concurrent-wrong-codes` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-AUTH-707260`, `API-AUTH-692907`.\n\n### BE-AUTH-FR-015 Retention deletion\n**Given** challenges, send attempts and locks older than their retention period,\n**When** the retention sweep runs (`backend.auth.jobs`),\n**Then** challenges and send attempts older than 1 day and locks older than 5 days are deleted.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-019; `ASM-BE-303`.\n- **Verification.** `test-auth-retention-sweep` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Open.** Clock basis `ASM-BE-407` / `GAP-BE-603`. Mechanism `GAP-BE-101`. Users `GAP-BE-600`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-AUTH-707260` | POST | `/auth/otp/send` | `auth` | 200 | 400, 415, 500, 503 | Request a 6-digit email one-time code for sign-in. Accepts at most 3 requests per email within a trailing 10 minutes (every request counts); further requests return 415 and create no code and send no email. Not refused during an account lock, but still subject to the resend limit. Response must not reveal whether the email has a User. |\n| `API-AUTH-692907` | POST | `/auth/otp/verify` | `auth` | 200 | 400, 415, 500 | Verify a 6-digit email one-time code. A correct, unexpired, unused code on an unlocked account succeeds, consumes the code (single use, safe under concurrent submission) and creates the User on first successful verification. Locked account (15 minutes after 5 wrong codes), wrong code, expired code (older than 10 minutes) and already-used code are each refused with 415, distinguished only by the error code. Success status and credential returned are not fixed by inputs (GAP-BE-604, Q-BE-012). |\n\n### `API-AUTH-707260` \u2014 POST `/auth/otp/send`\n\nRequest a 6-digit email one-time code for sign-in. Accepts at most 3 requests per email within a trailing 10 minutes (every request counts); further requests return 415 and create no code and send no email. Not refused during an account lock, but still subject to the resend limit. Response must not reveal whether the email has a User.\n\n**Request:** \n- `email` (string, required): Email address to which the code is sent. Compared in normalised form (ASM-BE-015). Personal data; never logged unredacted. Format validation rule is unstated (GAP-BE-035).\n**Response:** none\n\n### `API-AUTH-692907` \u2014 POST `/auth/otp/verify`\n\nVerify a 6-digit email one-time code. A correct, unexpired, unused code on an unlocked account succeeds, consumes the code (single use, safe under concurrent submission) and creates the User on first successful verification. Locked account (15 minutes after 5 wrong codes), wrong code, expired code (older than 10 minutes) and already-used code are each refused with 415, distinguished only by the error code. Success status and credential returned are not fixed by inputs (GAP-BE-604, Q-BE-012).\n\n**Request:** \n- `email` (string, required): Email address the code was sent to. Compared in normalised form (ASM-BE-015). Personal data.\n- `code` (string, required): The 6-digit numeric one-time code received by email (BR-029). Sent as a string so leading zeros survive.\n**Response:** \n- `credential` (string): Credential granting authenticated access after successful verification. Form (body token, cookie or other) and lifetime are undecided (Q-BE-012, GAP-BE-302); may instead be delivered outside the body.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\nProject-level invariants that bind this module: `INV-BE-002` (single use), `INV-BE-003` (User created only on successful verification), `INV-BE-004` (expiry), `INV-BE-005` (no success while locked), `INV-BE-006` (no code beyond the limit). They are defined in `backend.domain-model` and not restated.\n\nModule invariants, holding after every operation:\n\n- `INV-BE-010`. A verify refused because of a lock changes neither `failed_count`, `locked_until`, nor any challenge. [BR-022; `ASM-BE-408`]\n- `INV-BE-011`. Under any number of concurrent submissions of one correct code, exactly one succeeds. [BR-023; BE-AUTH-FR-014]\n- `INV-BE-012`. `failed_count` is between 0 and 4. `locked_until` is set only by the transition on the 5th wrong code, and is exactly that instant + 15 minutes. [BR-022, BR-025; `ASM-BE-404`]\n- `INV-BE-013`. For any email, accepted `send_attempts` rows within any trailing 10 minutes number at most 3. [BR-028; BR-032]\n- `INV-BE-014`. For every challenge, `expires_at` = `issued_at` + 10 minutes. [BR-024]\n- `INV-BE-015`. No response, log entry or error from either operation discloses whether the email has a User, and no log entry holds an unredacted email or a code. [`ASM-BE-400`; NFR-018, rule text corrupted, `GAP-BE-104`]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\n**Callers.** Both operations are used before a credential exists, so the caller is unauthenticated. No input defines API roles (`GAP-BE-085`), and by default a role has no permission an input does not grant (`ASM-BE-302`). Whether the two operations accept any caller is therefore a proposal, not a fact: the permission matrix is limited to the table below.\n\n| Role | `API-AUTH-707260` | `API-AUTH-692907` |\n|---|---|---|\n| unauthenticated caller | permitted, subject to eligibility | permitted, subject to eligibility |\n| authenticated caller | not stated | not stated |\n\n**Eligibility.** Who may request, verify or receive a code is `Q-BE-003` (blocking), because BR-030 is corrupted. `CON-BE-003` (Critical) keeps it open. Until answered, no eligibility check is specified and the operations above are the only access defined.\n\n**Ownership predicate.** No resource owned by this module is user-scoped. State is keyed by normalised email and is reachable only through the two operations. There is no read endpoint for challenges, attempts, locks or users.\n\n**Email transport.** Whether the address may leave the EU is `Q-BE-005` and `CON-BE-006`.\n\n**Unblocked by.** Answers to `Q-BE-003` and `Q-BE-005` and the resolution of `CON-BE-003` and `CON-BE-006`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n| Job | Work | Period | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `auth-retention-sweep` | delete `otp_challenges` with `issued_at` older than 1 day, `send_attempts` with `requested_at` older than 1 day, and `login_locks` with `updated_at` older than 5 days | frequency not stated | NFR-019; `ASM-BE-303`, `ASM-BE-407`; `GAP-BE-101` |\n\n- **Idempotency.** The sweep is idempotent: re-running deletes nothing further. A sweep does not remove a challenge that is still within its 10 minutes or a lock still in force, since the retention periods are longer.\n- **Retries.** A failed sweep is retried on the next run. No retry policy is stated for the mechanism (`GAP-BE-101`).\n- **Operation idempotency.** `API-AUTH-707260` is not idempotent: each accepted request creates a challenge and counts toward the limit. No idempotency key is supplied by any input, and a client retry therefore consumes quota (`GAP-BE-619`). `API-AUTH-692907` is safe to replay for a consumed code: it is refused as used (BE-AUTH-FR-006).\n- **No other scheduled work.** The lock lifts by time with no job (BE-AUTH-FR-011).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\n**Unit** (injectable clock; no network)\n- `test-auth-code-format`: generated code is 6 numeric digits. [BR-029]\n- `test-auth-expiry-arithmetic`: `expires_at` = `issued_at` + 10 minutes (`INV-BE-014`).\n- `test-auth-verify-decision-order`: the order of 3.4 over every combination of lock, consumed, expired and wrong.\n- `test-auth-email-normalisation`: equal addresses in differing form share one counter (`ASM-BE-015`).\n- `test-auth-log-redaction` (BE-AUTH-FR-013).\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16; email transport faked)\n- Send: `test-auth-send-issues-code`, `test-auth-send-limit-415`, `test-auth-send-while-locked`, `test-auth-send-window-slides`, `test-auth-refused-send-writes-no-row`.\n- Verify: `test-auth-verify-success`, `test-auth-verify-expired`, `test-auth-verify-expiry-boundary`, `test-auth-verify-single-use`, `test-auth-verify-wrong-counts`, `test-auth-lock-on-fifth-wrong`, `test-auth-verify-locked-correct-code`, `test-auth-lock-expires`, `test-auth-verify-no-challenge-not-counted`.\n- Concurrency: `test-auth-verify-concurrent-single-use`, `test-auth-concurrent-send-limit`, `test-auth-concurrent-wrong-codes`.\n- Data: `test-auth-user-created-on-first-verify-only`, `test-auth-retention-sweep`, `test-auth-constraints` (the CHECK constraints of the data model).\n\n**Contract** (against the published OpenAPI document)\n- `test-auth-contract-send-documented`, `test-auth-contract-verify-documented`: both operations appear with their 400 and 415 responses. [BR-012; NFR-008; US-004-03]\n- `test-auth-contract-error-codes-distinct`: the four verify refusals and the send refusal carry distinct error codes under status 415 (BE-AUTH-FR-010).\n- `test-auth-no-user-disclosure` (BE-AUTH-FR-012): responses for an existing and a non-existing address are indistinguishable in status, body shape and error code.\n- Success status, success body and credential assertions are not written until `GAP-BE-604` and `Q-BE-012` are closed.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.gaps | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\n### Open items from the project spec that bind this module\n\n| ID | Why it matters here |\n|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-003` (blocking) | who may request, verify or receive a code |\n| `Q-BE-005` (blocking) | email transport region; whether the address may leave the EU |\n| `Q-BE-012` (blocking) | the credential a successful verification returns |\n| `CON-BE-003` (Critical) | login eligibility and note access left open |\n| `CON-BE-006` (Critical) | email address possibly leaving the EU against BR-001 |\n| `CON-BE-011` | \"resend\" versus \"request\" counting; reading used: every request counts |\n| `GAP-BE-024`, `ASM-BE-305` | wrong-code counting: per email, no window, cleared on success |\n| `GAP-BE-031`, `GAP-BE-033`, `GAP-BE-035` | normalisation, at-rest treatment, input validation |\n| `GAP-BE-083` | effect of a new code on earlier codes |\n| `GAP-BE-085`, `GAP-BE-089` | existing authentication; libraries |\n| `GAP-BE-101`, `GAP-BE-603`, `GAP-BE-600` | retention mechanism, clock basis, Users |\n| `GAP-BE-104`, `GAP-BE-304` | log treatment; encryption at rest |\n| `GAP-BE-301`, `GAP-BE-302` | no latency target; credential lifetime |\n| `GAP-BE-306` | the instant exactly 10 minutes after issue |\n| `GAP-BE-601`, `GAP-BE-604` | order of refusals; success status and body |\n| `GAP-BE-602` | the content of three inbound handoffs was not supplied |\n\n### New gaps raised by this module\n\n- `GAP-BE-617`. The source and quality of randomness for the 6-digit code is not stated. Closing artefact: a security requirement for code generation.\n- `GAP-BE-618`. Behaviour when the email transport fails after the challenge and send attempt are stored is not stated: whether the attempt still counts, whether the caller is told. Closing artefact: a decision from the product owner and a transport failure policy.\n- `GAP-BE-619`. No idempotency key or de-duplication for `API-AUTH-707260`; a client retry counts against the limit of 3. Closing artefact: a decision on client retry behaviour.\n- `GAP-BE-620`. Whether the `users` table is shared with, or separate from, the existing authentication's accounts is not stated. Closing artefact: the existing authentication description (`GAP-BE-085`).\n- `GAP-BE-621`. No abuse control beyond the per-email limits (per source address, global) is stated, and the per-email limit alone does not stop one caller trying many addresses. Closing artefact: a security requirement.\n- `GAP-BE-622`. The rate-limit class for the two operations is not stated beyond BR-021, BR-028 and BR-032. Closing artefact: a rate-limit policy.\n- `GAP-BE-623`. Whether the 5th wrong code returns the invalid-code or the locked-account refusal is not stated. Default in BE-AUTH-FR-008: invalid-code.\n\n### Assumptions introduced by this module\n\n- `ASM-BE-401`. Column types: uuid keys, text email, timestamptz instants, integer counter, bigint identity. Reason: PostgreSQL 16 is the platform and no types are supplied. Confirmed by: a platform decision.\n- `ASM-BE-402`. A code is expired only when the instant is strictly after `expires_at`. Reason: BR-024 and US-006-06 say \"after\"; BR-020 and BR-026 say \"expires\" and BR-033's boundary clause is corrupted (`GAP-BE-306`). Confirmed by: a decision on `GAP-BE-306`.\n- `ASM-BE-403`. A refused send writes no `send_attempts` row. Reason: otherwise a caller who keeps retrying is never released from the window. Confirmed by: a rule from the product owner.\n- `ASM-BE-404`. `failed_count` is reset to 0 on entering a lock, so it ranges 0 to 4. Reason: otherwise the first wrong code after the lock would re-lock. Confirmed by: a rule from the product owner.\n- `ASM-BE-405`. Only the latest challenge for an email is checked at verification. Reason: `GAP-BE-083` is open and one live code is the narrower reading. Consequence: submitting an older code after a newer one was issued is judged against the newer challenge. Confirmed by: a decision on `GAP-BE-083`.\n- `ASM-BE-406`. Only a wrong code against the latest unexpired, unconsumed challenge counts toward the lock; expired, used and no-challenge refusals do not. Reason: BR-022 says \"wrong codes\"; an expired or used code is refused, not wrong. Confirmed by: a rule from the product owner.\n- `ASM-BE-407`. Retention clocks: `issued_at` for challenges, `requested_at` for send attempts, `updated_at` for locks. Reason: NFR-019 gives periods but no basis (`GAP-BE-603`). Confirmed by: a decision on `GAP-BE-603`.\n- `ASM-BE-408`. A verify refused because of a lock changes no state. Reason: BR-022 refuses verification during the lock and states no extension. Confirmed by: a rule from the product owner.\n- `ASM-BE-409`. The verify decision order of 3.4, and that a verify with no challenge for the email returns OTP_CODE_INVALID and is not counted. Reason: `GAP-BE-601`; this does not reveal whether the address exists (`ASM-BE-400`). Confirmed by: a decision on `GAP-BE-601`.\n\nNo new `CON-` or `Q-` is raised by this module. Contradictions and questions that touch it are the ones listed above, defined in the project spec.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-006-06", "US-004-03"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/notes-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/notes-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: notes\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [Q-BE-003, CON-BE-003]\nscope: [API-NOTES-232390, API-NOTES-045877, API-NOTES-847551, API-NOTES-359413, API-NOTES-150096, API-NOTES-810640, API-NOTES-917869]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.purpose | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The `notes` module owns the note-taking data (NOTE, TAG, NOTE_TAG) and these operations: create, get, partial edit, add tag, remove tag, archive, unarchive. [backend.module-map; stories.md US-002-01 to US-002-06]\n\n1. **Create** a note with a mandatory non-empty title and body; `pinned` defaults to false and may be set true on create. `API-NOTES-232390`. [BR-005, BR-018; AC-002-01-01 to AC-002-01-03]\n2. **Get** one note by id with its tags, archive status and pinned flag, archived notes included. `API-NOTES-045877`. [US-002-06]\n3. **Edit** a note by partial update; a field not sent is never overwritten. `API-NOTES-847551`. [BR-006, BR-018; US-002-02]\n4. **Tag** a note and remove a tag. Adding an existing tag creates no duplicate. `API-NOTES-359413`, `API-NOTES-150096`. [US-002-03]\n5. **Archive** and **unarchive** a note. Archiving hides the note from active views and never deletes data. Unarchive restores full prior state. `API-NOTES-810640`, `API-NOTES-917869`. [BR-007, BR-008; US-002-04, US-002-05]\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- Search, list ordering and pagination, including the pinned-first order (`INV-BE-001`). These belong to `search`. [backend.module-map]\n- Sign-in, codes, locks and the credential check itself. These belong to `auth`. `notes` only depends on `auth` to validate the caller on protected routes. [backend.module-map]\n- The OpenAPI document and its coverage (`api-docs`), and rejection of attachment, real-time and mobile calls (`scope-guardrail`).\n- The global error envelope and registry, owned by `backend.error-model`; this module uses it.\n- Note ownership columns and the note access rule. These are `BLOCKED` on `Q-BE-003` and `CON-BE-003`.\n- Retention deletion of notes. NFR-019 states notes are not deleted by retention. [NFR-019]\n- Any user interface (the web-app surface).\n\n**Operations owned.** The seven ids in the frontmatter `scope`, referenced by id only.\n\n**Identifier note.** This module mints from its reserved block: `ASM-BE-451` onward, `GAP-BE-667` onward, `INV-BE-060` onward, `Q-BE-351` onward. It mints no `CON-`; it cites `CON-BE-003`. All other ids are cited from the project spec.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\nPostgreSQL 16 is the platform. [platform.md] No input states column types, nullability or constraints, so those below are a proposal (`ASM-BE-451`). Entity and column names follow `backend.domain-model`. No ORM or migration tool is named (`GAP-BE-089`). The tables are created by migration M001. [backend.migration-plan]\n\n**notes**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | primary key; server-assigned (`ASM-BE-453`) |\n| `title` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (btrim(title) <> '')` (`ASM-BE-454`); maximum length `GAP-BE-035` |\n| `body` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (btrim(body) <> '')` (`ASM-BE-454`); maximum length `GAP-BE-035` |\n| `status` | text | NOT NULL | `'active'` | `CHECK (status IN ('active','archived'))` (`ASM-BE-452`) |\n| `pinned` | boolean | NOT NULL | `false` | `ASM-BE-034`; [BR-018] |\n| `created_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | |\n| `updated_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | `now()` | set on every change to the note row or its tags (`ASM-BE-455`) |\n\nNo ownership column is defined. It is `BLOCKED` on `Q-BE-003` and `CON-BE-003` (migration M005). [backend.domain-model]\n\n**tags**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `name` | text | NOT NULL | none | primary key; format and case rule `GAP-BE-035` |\n\n**note_tags**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `note_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | foreign key to `notes(id)` |\n| `tag_name` | text | NOT NULL | none | foreign key to `tags(name)` |\n| (pair) | | | | primary key `(note_id, tag_name)`; this is what prevents a duplicate tag entry (`INV-BE-061`) |\n\n**Referential integrity.**\n\n- `note_tags.note_id` references `notes(id)` with `ON DELETE RESTRICT`. Notes are never hard-deleted, so no cascade is needed. [BR-007; NFR-019]\n- `note_tags.tag_name` references `tags(name)` with `ON DELETE RESTRICT`. Whether an unused tag is removed is `GAP-BE-035`.\n\n**Indexes.**\n\n- Primary key on `notes(id)` serves get, patch, tag and archive operations.\n- btree on `note_tags(tag_name)` serves the tag-filtered lookup that `search` performs (read-only). [BR-010; US-003-02]\n- Indexes for keyword search are owned by `search` (migration M003). [backend.migration-plan]\n\n**Time.** Every instant is `timestamptz`.\n\n**Encryption and PII.** No input states encryption at rest (`GAP-BE-304`), so none is claimed. Note content may contain personal data; no PII handling policy exists (`GAP-BE-017`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\nEntity: `notes`, column `status`. States: `active`, `archived`. Neither is terminal: the lifecycle is reversible, and no hard-delete state exists. [BR-007, BR-008; NFR-019] There is no `deleted` state.\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `active` | `API-NOTES-232390` | title and body non-empty | row created with `pinned` as supplied or `false` (`ASM-BE-452`) |\n| `active` | `archived` | `API-NOTES-810640` | note exists | `status` set; all data intact; `updated_at` set |\n| `archived` | `active` | `API-NOTES-917869` | note exists | `status` set; all prior data preserved |\n| `active` | `active` | `API-NOTES-847551`, `API-NOTES-359413`, `API-NOTES-150096` | note exists; valid input | edit, pin or tag change |\n| `archived` | `archived` | `API-NOTES-847551`, `API-NOTES-359413`, `API-NOTES-150096` | note exists; valid input | same as for an active note (`ASM-BE-456`) |\n\n**Illegal transitions.**\n\n| Attempt | Result | Status and code |\n|---|---|---|\n| archive an already-archived note | refused, no data modified | conflict/error; status not stated (`GAP-BE-309`); proposed code `NOTE_ALREADY_ARCHIVED`. [US-002-04] |\n| unarchive an already-active note | refused | not stated (`GAP-BE-667`) |\n| any operation on a non-existent note id | refused, no data modified | 404 `NOTE_NOT_FOUND`. [US-002-02, US-002-05, US-002-06] |\n\n**Badge derivation.** The archived/active indicator a consumer shows is a function of `status` alone. Nothing else feeds it. [US-006-01, US-002-06]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-001 Create a note\n**Given** a payload with a non-empty `title` and `body`,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** a note is stored with a unique id, `created_at` and `updated_at`, `status` `active` and `pinned` `false`, and 201 is returned with the created note.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; BR-005, BR-018; AC-002-01-01.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-defaults` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n- **Status.** Field limits `GAP-BE-035`. Access check `BLOCKED` on `Q-BE-003`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-002 Create a pinned note\n**Given** a valid payload with `pinned` true,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** 201 is returned and the stored note has `pinned` true.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; BR-018; AC-002-01-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-pinned` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-003 Reject an invalid create\n**Given** a payload missing `title`, or with an empty or null `title` or `body`,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` with a validation error is returned and no note is persisted.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; BR-005; AC-002-01-03; NFR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-missing-title`, `test-notes-create-empty-body`, `test-notes-create-null-fields` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n- **Status.** Whitespace-only counts as empty (`ASM-BE-454`).\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-004 Get a note\n**Given** an existing note id,\n**When** `API-NOTES-045877` is called,\n**Then** 200 is returned with the full note, including its tags, `status` and `pinned`. An archived note is returned too.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-06; NFR-005.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-get-active`, `test-notes-get-archived` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-045877`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-005 Get a missing note\n**Given** an id for a note that does not exist or never existed,\n**When** `API-NOTES-045877` is called,\n**Then** 404 `NOTE_NOT_FOUND` is returned with no partial data.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-06.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-get-missing` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-045877`.\n- **Status.** A malformed (non-uuid) id returns 400 or 404; not stated (`GAP-BE-668`).\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-006 Partial edit\n**Given** an existing note and valid updated fields,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** 200 is returned with the updated note and a new `updated_at`, and every field not sent is unchanged.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02; BR-006; AC-002-02-01.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-partial`, `test-notes-patch-preserves-unsent` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n- **Status.** Accepted fields `ASM-BE-457`. PUT is not offered (`GAP-BE-309`).\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-007 Pin and unpin through PATCH\n**Given** a PATCH containing only `pinned`,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** 200 is returned, `pinned` is updated, and title, body and tags are unchanged.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02; BR-018; AC-002-02-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-pinned-only` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-008 Edit a missing note\n**Given** a non-existent note id,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** 404 `NOTE_NOT_FOUND` is returned and no data is modified.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02; AC-002-02-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-missing` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-009 Reject an invalid edit\n**Given** a PATCH that sets `title` or `body` to empty or null, sends an empty payload, or contains an unknown field,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` is returned and the note is unchanged.\n- **Traces to.** BR-005 and BR-006 by derivation (`ASM-BE-457`); NFR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-empty-title`, `test-notes-patch-unknown-field` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-010 Add a tag\n**Given** an existing note and a tag name,\n**When** `API-NOTES-359413` is called,\n**Then** 200 is returned and the note's tag list includes the tag.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-add` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-359413`.\n- **Status.** Tag format `GAP-BE-035`. Request body shape is in the operation record.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-011 Add a duplicate tag\n**Given** a tag already on the note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-359413` is called with the same tag,\n**Then** 200 is returned and no duplicate entry is created (`INV-BE-061`).\n- **Traces to.** US-002-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-add-duplicate`, `test-notes-tag-add-concurrent` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-359413`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-012 Remove a tag\n**Given** an existing note carrying the tag,\n**When** `API-NOTES-150096` is called,\n**Then** the tag is removed from the note and the note's other tags are unchanged.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-03 (add or remove); US-006-04 consumer view.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-remove` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-150096`.\n- **Status.** Success status, and the result when the note lacks the tag, are `GAP-BE-669`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-013 Archive a note\n**Given** an active, existing note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-810640` is called,\n**Then** 200 is returned, `status` becomes `archived`, and all note data remains intact.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-04; BR-007.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-archive`, `test-notes-archive-preserves-data` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-810640`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-014 Archive an archived note\n**Given** an already-archived note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-810640` is called,\n**Then** a conflict/error response indicating it is already archived is returned and nothing is modified.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-04.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-archive-twice` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-810640`.\n- **Status.** Status code `GAP-BE-309`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-015 Unarchive a note\n**Given** an archived note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-917869` is called,\n**Then** 200 is returned, `status` becomes `active`, and all original data is preserved.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-05; BR-008.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-unarchive`, `test-notes-archive-roundtrip` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-917869`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-016 Unarchive a missing note\n**Given** a note id that does not exist,\n**When** `API-NOTES-917869` is called,\n**Then** 404 `NOTE_NOT_FOUND` is returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-unarchive-missing` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-917869`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-017 Archive and tag operations on a missing note\n**Given** a non-existent note id,\n**When** `API-NOTES-810640`, `API-NOTES-359413` or `API-NOTES-150096` is called,\n**Then** 404 `NOTE_NOT_FOUND` is returned and nothing is modified.\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02 and US-002-05 (the stated 404 pattern); `ASM-BE-458`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-archive-missing`, `test-notes-tag-missing` (planned).\n- **API.** the three named ids.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-018 Injection safety\n**Given** any title, body or tag value,\n**When** it is stored or read,\n**Then** it is validated and handled so that it cannot alter a query or be executed.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-injection-strings` (planned), plus the security scan per release.\n- **API.** all seven ids.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-019 Concurrent edits\n**Given** two PATCH requests to the same note processed at about the same time,\n**When** both are applied,\n**Then** no live broadcast or merge logic runs, and a standard last-write or conflict response results.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-concurrent` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n- **Status.** Which outcome is `GAP-BE-309`; default `ASM-BE-459`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-NOTES-232390` | POST | `/notes` | `notes` | 201 | 400, 401, 403 | Create a note with a mandatory non-empty title and body, optionally pinned (US-002-01, BR-005, BR-018) |\n| `API-NOTES-045877` | GET | `/notes/{id}` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404 | Get a single note by id, including archived notes (US-002-06) |\n| `API-NOTES-847551` | PATCH | `/notes/{id}` | `notes` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 404 | Partially edit a note; fields not sent are never overwritten (US-002-02, BR-006, BR-018) |\n| `API-NOTES-810640` | POST | `/notes/{id}/archive` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404, 409 | Archive a note without deleting data (US-002-04, BR-007) |\n| `API-NOTES-359413` | POST | `/notes/{id}/tags` | `notes` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 404 | Add a tag to a note; an existing tag creates no duplicate (US-002-03) |\n| `API-NOTES-150096` | DELETE | `/notes/{id}/tags/{name}` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404 | Remove a tag from a note (US-002-03) |\n| `API-NOTES-917869` | POST | `/notes/{id}/unarchive` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404 | Restore an archived note to active with all data preserved (US-002-05, BR-008) |\n\n### `API-NOTES-232390` \u2014 POST `/notes`\n\nCreate a note with a mandatory non-empty title and body, optionally pinned (US-002-01, BR-005, BR-018)\n\n**Request:** \n- `title` (string, required): Note title; mandatory and non-empty at creation (BR-005)\n- `body` (string, required): Note body; mandatory and non-empty at creation (BR-005)\n- `pinned` (boolean): Whether the note is pinned; defaults to false (BR-018)\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Identifier of the created note\n- `title` (string, required): Note title\n- `body` (string, required): Note body\n- `status` (string, required): Note status, active or archived; a new note is active\n- `pinned` (boolean, required): Whether the note is pinned\n- `tags` (array, required): Tags applied to the note\n- `created_at` (string, required): Creation timestamp\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n\n### `API-NOTES-045877` \u2014 GET `/notes/{id}`\n\nGet a single note by id, including archived notes (US-002-06)\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `title` (string, required): Note title\n- `body` (string, required): Note body\n- `status` (string, required): active or archived\n- `pinned` (boolean, required): Whether the note is pinned\n- `tags` (array, required): Tags applied to the note\n- `created_at` (string, required): Creation timestamp\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n\n### `API-NOTES-847551` \u2014 PATCH `/notes/{id}`\n\nPartially edit a note; fields not sent are never overwritten (US-002-02, BR-006, BR-018)\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier\n- `title` (string): New title; omitted fields stay unchanged\n- `body` (string): New body; omitted fields stay unchanged\n- `pinned` (boolean): Pin or unpin the note (BR-018)\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `title` (string, required): Note title after edit\n- `body` (string, required): Note body after edit\n- `status` (string, required): active or archived\n- `pinned` (boolean, required): Whether the note is pinned\n- `tags` (array, required): Tags applied to the note\n- `created_at` (string, required): Creation timestamp\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n\n### `API-NOTES-810640` \u2014 POST `/notes/{id}/archive`\n\nArchive a note without deleting data (US-002-04, BR-007)\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `status` (string, required): archived after success\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n\n### `API-NOTES-359413` \u2014 POST `/notes/{id}/tags`\n\nAdd a tag to a note; an existing tag creates no duplicate (US-002-03)\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier\n- `name` (string, required): Tag name to apply; format rules are unspecified\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `tags` (array, required): Tags now applied to the note, without duplicates\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n\n### `API-NOTES-150096` \u2014 DELETE `/notes/{id}/tags/{name}`\n\nRemove a tag from a note (US-002-03)\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier\n- `name` (string, required): Path parameter: tag name to remove\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `tags` (array, required): Tags remaining on the note\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n\n### `API-NOTES-917869` \u2014 POST `/notes/{id}/unarchive`\n\nRestore an archived note to active with all data preserved (US-002-05, BR-008)\n\n**Request:** \n- `id` (string, required): Path parameter: note identifier\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier\n- `status` (string, required): active after success\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\nThe project invariants `INV-BE-001` (pinned-first order, enforced by `search`), `INV-BE-008` and `INV-BE-009` apply here and are defined in `backend.domain-model`. This module adds:\n\n- `INV-BE-060`. Every stored note has a non-empty `title` and `body`, and `status` is `active` or `archived`. [BR-005]\n- `INV-BE-061`. A note carries a given tag at most once. [US-002-03]\n- `INV-BE-062`. No operation of this module deletes a note row. Archive sets `status` and nothing else is removed. [BR-007; NFR-019]\n- `INV-BE-063`. After archive then unarchive, title, body, tags, `pinned` and `created_at` equal their values before the archive. [BR-008]\n- `INV-BE-064`. A failed or refused operation (validation, 404, conflict) modifies no data. [AC-002-01-03, AC-002-02-03]\n- `INV-BE-065`. `updated_at` is never earlier than `created_at`, and changes whenever a field or tag of the note changes (`ASM-BE-455`). [AC-002-02-01]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## 7. Authorisation\n\n**Blocked.** The role-by-operation matrix and the ownership predicate cannot be written.\n\n- **Roles.** No input defines API caller roles; the existing authentication is unnamed (`GAP-BE-085`). `backend.auth-model` is `BLOCKED` for the same reason.\n- **Ownership.** No input relates a note to a user (`GAP-BE-003`). BR-031, which sets the note access rule, is corrupted. The default is a shared workspace (`Q-BE-001`), which `CON-BE-003` (Critical) calls into question.\n- **Credential.** What the caller presents is `Q-BE-012`.\n- **Default.** Until an input grants a permission, a role has none (`ASM-BE-302`). Authentication and authorisation failures return 401 and 403, documented per endpoint. [US-004-03]\n\n**Unblocked by:** answers to `Q-BE-003` and `Q-BE-012`, the resolution of `CON-BE-003`, and a statement of the existing authentication method and its roles. Note ownership columns follow in migration M005.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.jobs | status: UNKNOWN -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n**Scheduled work.** None. Notes are not deleted by retention (NFR-019), and archiving is an explicit user action, not a sweep. [BR-007]\n\n**Retries.** None specified. A database error returns a server error; the response is not an input-defined status (`GAP-BE-670`).\n\n**Idempotency.** No input states idempotency keys.\n\n| Operation | Behaviour |\n|---|---|\n| `API-NOTES-232390` | not idempotent; each call creates a note. De-duplication by key is not stated (`GAP-BE-671`). |\n| `API-NOTES-847551` | the same body applied twice gives the same stored state; `updated_at` changes |\n| `API-NOTES-359413` | idempotent: a repeat returns 200 and creates no duplicate. [US-002-03] |\n| `API-NOTES-150096` | repeat behaviour `GAP-BE-669` |\n| `API-NOTES-810640` | a repeat is refused as already archived. [US-002-04] |\n| `API-NOTES-917869` | repeat behaviour `GAP-BE-667` |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\n**Unit.**\n\n- Title and body validation: missing, empty, null, whitespace-only (`ASM-BE-454`).\n- Partial-update merge keeps every unsent field (`INV-BE-008`).\n- Status transitions: allowed and refused combinations in `backend.notes.state-machine`.\n- Tag de-duplication.\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16, as the platform states; no test framework is named, `GAP-BE-089`).\n\n- Create, get, patch, tag, archive, unarchive against a real database, covering each status and error stated in `backend.notes.requirements`.\n- The roundtrip archive then unarchive preserves all data (`INV-BE-063`).\n- Concurrent duplicate tag add yields one `note_tags` row.\n- A refused operation leaves the row unchanged (`INV-BE-064`).\n- CRUD functional-test pass rate of 100% with zero data loss. [NFR-005]\n- Injection strings in title, body and tag. [NFR-012]\n\n**Contract.**\n\n- Every operation of this module appears in the published OpenAPI document with request and response schemas and 4xx errors. [BR-012, NFR-008]\n- Tagging operations carry example payloads. [NFR-009]\n- Responses match the error envelope and the registry in `backend.error-model`.\n\nAuthorisation tests are not planned until `backend.notes.authorization` is unblocked.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.gaps | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 10. Open items\n\n**Project items that bind this module** (defined in `backend.overview`, referenced here, not restated): `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-003`, `Q-BE-012`, `CON-BE-003`, `GAP-BE-003`, `GAP-BE-017`, `GAP-BE-035`, `GAP-BE-085`, `GAP-BE-089`, `GAP-BE-300`, `GAP-BE-304`, `GAP-BE-309`, `GAP-BE-310`, `ASM-BE-034`, `ASM-BE-300`, `ASM-BE-301`, `ASM-BE-302`.\n\n**Gaps raised by this module**\n\n| ID | Gap | Degrades | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-667` | Result of unarchiving an already-active note (status and code). | state-machine, FR for unarchive | a decision from the product owner |\n| `GAP-BE-668` | Response for a malformed (non-uuid) note id. | FR-005 and every `{id}` operation | a validation rule |\n| `GAP-BE-669` | Success status of tag removal, and the result when the note lacks the tag. | FR-012, jobs | a decision from the product owner |\n| `GAP-BE-670` | Status and body for an unexpected server error. | jobs | an error-handling policy |\n| `GAP-BE-671` | Whether create supports a client idempotency key. | jobs | a decision from the product owner |\n\n**Assumptions raised by this module**\n\n| ID | Assumption | Reasoning and what would confirm it |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-451` | Column types: uuid keys, text strings, boolean, timestamptz. | PostgreSQL 16 is the platform and no types are supplied. Confirm by a platform decision. |\n| `ASM-BE-452` | A new note has `status` `active`. | US-002-04 archives an \"active, existing note\"; the create criteria carry no status. Confirm by the product owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-453` | The note id is a server-assigned opaque uuid, never client-supplied. | AC-002-01-01 says the API returns a unique ID. Confirm by the product owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-454` | A title or body of whitespace only counts as empty. | BR-005 rejects \"empty/null\" payloads. Confirm by the product owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-455` | `updated_at` changes on any edit, pin change or tag change. | AC-002-02-01 returns \"an updated timestamp\"; tag changes are an edit of the note per US-002-02. Confirm by the product owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-456` | Edit, pin and tag changes on an archived note behave as on an active note. | No input forbids them. Confirm by the product owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-457` | PATCH accepts only `title`, `body` and `pinned`. An empty payload, an unknown field, or an empty or null title or body is 400. Tags change only through the tag operations. | The supplied operation records list those fields; BR-005's non-empty rule is extended to edits to avoid content loss, in the spirit of BR-006. Confirm by the product owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-458` | Archive and tag operations return 404 for a missing note, like the stated cases. | US-002-02, US-002-05 and US-002-06 state 404 for the other operations. Confirm by the product owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-459` | A concurrent PATCH is last-write-wins on the supplied fields. | US-005-03 allows \"last-write/conflict\". Last-write is the narrower, non-blocking reading. It is emitted blocked rather than omitted: a missing section is invisible, a blocked one is a work item.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-SEARCH-580505` | GET | `/notes/search` | `search` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 503 | Search notes and tags with pagination and ordering; pinned notes precede unpinned notes in results. Path and parameter names are proposed (no input supplies them, see GAP-BE-303). |\n\n### `API-SEARCH-580505` \u2014 GET `/notes/search`\n\nSearch notes and tags with pagination and ordering; pinned notes precede unpinned notes in results. Path and parameter names are proposed (no input supplies them, see GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** \n- `q` (string): Free-text search term matched against note title, body and tag names. Proposed name; format and length limits unstated (GAP-BE-035).\n- `tag` (string): Restrict results to notes carrying this tag name. Proposed name; tag format unstated (GAP-BE-035).\n- `include_archived` (boolean): When true, archived notes are included in results; an invalid value returns a 400 validation failure.\n- `page` (integer): Page number to return. Proposed name; invalid values return 400.\n- `page_size` (integer): Number of results per page. Default and maximum not stated (GAP-BE-500); NFR-007 names page sizes of 100. Invalid values return 400.\n**Response:** \n- `items` (array, required): Matching notes for the requested page, with every pinned note before every unpinned note (INV-BE-001). Empty array with 200 when nothing matches.\n- `page` (integer, required): The page number returned.\n- `page_size` (integer, required): The page size applied to this response.\n- `total` (integer, required): Total number of notes matching the query across all pages.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\n- `INV-BE-001` (cited from the project spec). Every pinned note precedes every unpinned note in results. [BR-019]\n- `INV-BE-110`. The operation changes no data: it writes no row to any table. [stories.md EP-003 (read-only operation); backend.module-map]\n- `INV-BE-111`. Without `include_archived=true`, no archived note appears in results. [BR-009]\n- `INV-BE-112`. No response contains an unbounded result set. [BR-011]\n- `INV-BE-113`. A note appears at most once per response, whatever the number of matching tags (`ASM-BE-502`).\n- `INV-BE-114`. A request that fails validation returns 400 and runs no query. (`ASM-BE-503`)\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\n**Blocked.** The role-by-operation matrix and the ownership predicate cannot be written.\n\n- No input defines API roles; the existing authentication method is unnamed (`GAP-BE-085`).\n- Whether a caller sees all notes or only their own depends on `Q-BE-001` and `Q-BE-003`; `CON-BE-003` (Critical) keeps the note access rule open. `backend.auth-model` is itself `BLOCKED`.\n- Until an input grants a permission, a role has none (`ASM-BE-302`).\n- The ownership predicate for search results, when decided, must be applied to the result set and to the total count, so the count discloses no note the caller may not read (`ASM-BE-504`).\n\n**Unblocked by:** answers to `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-003` and `Q-BE-012`, resolution of `CON-BE-003`, and a statement of the existing authentication method.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n- **Scheduled work.** None stated by any input. If the full-text index is a stored or materialised structure that needs refresh, that is `GAP-BE-725`; with a PostgreSQL expression index (`ASM-BE-501`) no job is needed.\n- **Retries.** The operation does not retry server-side. A client may retry on 503 after the retry-after interval (`GAP-BE-724`).\n- **Idempotency.** `GET` is safe and idempotent; no idempotency key is needed. [INV-BE-110]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\n**Unit.**\n- Parameter parsing and validation for `q`, `tags`, `include_archived`, `page`, `limit` (FR-003, 005, 007, 010, 014).\n- Query construction: pinned-first then relevance ordering; default `status=active` filter.\n\n**Integration (PostgreSQL 16, seeded data).**\n- All FR-001 to FR-014 and FR-017 tests named in `backend.search.requirements`.\n- A note with several matching tags appears once (`INV-BE-113`).\n- A simulated index failure returns 503 with retry-after (FR-016).\n- No write occurs during a search (`INV-BE-110`).\n\n**Load.**\n- `test-search-load-p95`: 50 requests per second, page size up to 100, dataset of 100k notes; assert p95 < 300 ms. [NFR-006, NFR-007; US-003-03]\n\n**Contract.**\n- The response and error shapes match the published OpenAPI document for `API-SEARCH-580505`, including 400, 401, 403 and 503, and example payloads include an empty result. [NFR-008, NFR-009; US-004-05]\n- Authorization tests are `BLOCKED` with section 7.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.gaps | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\n**Open questions (this module).**\n\n| ID | Class | Question |\n|---|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-401` | degradable | Is a search with `tags` and no `q` allowed? US-003-01 requires `q`; US-003-02 supplies `tags` alone. Default: allowed when at least one of `q` or `tags` is present, 400 when neither is (`ASM-BE-505`). |\n\n**Gaps.**\n\n| ID | Statement | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-717` | Text-search configuration (language, stemming, ranking) is not stated. | a decision on language and ranking |\n| `GAP-BE-718` | Which module creates the search indexes is not stated. | a decision on ownership |\n| `GAP-BE-719` | With several tags, whether a note needs all or any is not stated. | a rule from the product owner |\n| `GAP-BE-720` | The separator for several tags in `tags` is not stated. | a parameter format decision |\n| `GAP-BE-721` | \"Malformed filter combination\" (US-003-04) is undefined. | a list of invalid combinations |\n| `GAP-BE-722` | Page base, response field names and total-count field name are not stated. | response shape decision |\n| `GAP-BE-723` | Accepted literals for `include_archived` are not stated. | a parameter format decision |\n| `GAP-BE-724` | Retry-after value and the timeout before 503 are not stated. | a resilience rule |\n| `GAP-BE-725` | Whether any index refresh job is needed is not stated. | the index technology decision |\n\n**Open items cited from the project spec.** `GAP-BE-001`, `GAP-BE-017`, `GAP-BE-035`, `GAP-BE-085`, `GAP-BE-303`, `GAP-BE-304`, `GAP-BE-310`, `GAP-BE-500`; `CON-BE-003` (Critical); `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-003`, `Q-BE-012`; `ASM-BE-302`.\n\n**Assumptions.**\n\n| ID | Assumption |\n|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-501` | Search is implemented with PostgreSQL 16 full-text and btree indexes. PostgreSQL is the platform [platform.md]; the specific index types are proposals. Confirm with the platform decision. |\n| `ASM-BE-502` | Results are de-duplicated per note when several tags match. Stories imply a list of notes, not note-tag rows. |\n| `ASM-BE-503` | Validation precedes query execution. Standard behaviour implied by the 400 criteria. |\n| `ASM-BE-504` | The total count honours the same visibility rules as the results. Depends on the access rule. |\n| `ASM-BE-505` | Default for `Q-BE-401`: at least one of `q` or `tags` is required. |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-003-01", "US-003-02", "US-003-03", "US-003-04", "US-003-05", "US-003-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/residency-compliance-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/residency-compliance-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: residency-compliance\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [CON-BE-151, Q-BE-151, Q-BE-152]\nscope: [API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.purpose | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The `residency-compliance` module is the service-side evidence and enforcement point for EU data residency of `notes_api` infrastructure (database, compute, backup storage). [requirements.md BR-001; stories.md EP-001; backend.module-map] It owns:\n\n1. **Region validation.** Checking a resource's declared region against the approved EU region list, for provisioning requests, backup destinations and the CI/CD residency gate (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210`). [US-001-01, US-001-02, US-001-03; BR-001, BR-002]\n2. **Scanning.** Recording the region and compliance status of each provisioned resource (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`). [US-001-04, US-001-06]\n3. **Inventory.** Listing resources with region, status and last scan time (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444`). A resource type the scanner does not recognise is shown as `unverified`, never omitted. [AC-001-04-02]\n4. **Compliance report.** Per-resource region and pass/fail status plus an overall % compliant metric (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798`). [AC-001-04-01; NFR-004]\n5. **Launch gate evaluation.** A read-only computation from the current report (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`). [US-001-05; BR-003]\n6. **Drift alerts.** Raising and listing alerts when a running resource is found outside the approved regions (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`). [US-001-06; BR-004; NFR-003]\n7. **Data ownership.** INFRA_RESOURCE and DRIFT_ALERT, and the scan-run record this module needs to operate (`ASM-BE-557`). [backend.domain-model]\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- **Provisioning and backup execution.** Creating the database, compute or backups is done by infrastructure-as-code outside this service. [BR-001 Enforcement: \"Infrastructure-as-code region constraints\"] This module reports a validation result. It creates and rejects no resource itself.\n- **Pipeline wiring.** Failing the build or blocking a deploy is done by the pipeline acting on the validation result. Pipeline failure is not an HTTP response. [backend.error-model; AC-001-03-02]\n- **Release authorisation.** Whether a passed gate authorises release or Engineering Lead sign-off is required is `CON-BE-151` and `Q-BE-152`. No sign-off operation is defined here.\n- **Alert delivery channel and recipients.** Open in `GAP-BE-308`. This module records the alert and exposes it; delivery is not specified.\n- **Note data, login data, the global error envelope, the OpenAPI document and its Scope section.** These belong to `notes`, `auth`, `backend.error-model` and `api-docs`. `residency-compliance` has no outbound module dependencies. [backend.module-map]\n- **Backup scheduling, frequency and retention.** Not stated by any input (`GAP-BE-307`).\n- **Email transport region.** `Q-BE-005` and `CON-BE-006`. It is a residency question for the login flow, owned by `auth`.\n\n**Operations owned.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`, referenced by id only.\n\n**Identifier note.** This module mints from its reserved blocks: `ASM-BE-551` onward, `CON-BE-451` onward, `GAP-BE-767` onward, `INV-BE-160` onward. It mints no `Q-`. Other ids are cited from the project spec.\n\n**Inbound handoffs.** Three handoffs were delivered to this run (HANDOFF-WEBAPP-163212, HANDOFF-WEBAPP-200601, HANDOFF-WEBAPP-743710). Their content was not supplied (`GAP-BE-602`), so none can be answered here.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\nPostgreSQL 16 is the platform [platform.md]. No input supplies column types, nullability or constraints, so everything below is a proposal (`ASM-BE-551`). Entity names follow `backend.domain-model`. Whether these tables live in the `notes_api` database at all is `GAP-BE-305`.\n\n**approved_regions**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `region` | text | NOT NULL | none | primary key; exact-match comparison (`ASM-BE-561`) |\n| `added_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n\nThe contents are the approved EU region list. That list is not supplied (`Q-BE-151`, AC-001-01-01). No row is written in this spec.\n\n**infra_resources** (INFRA_RESOURCE)\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `resource_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | primary key |\n| `external_ref` | text | NOT NULL | none | unique; the resource's identifier in the hosting platform |\n| `resource_type` | text | NOT NULL | none | free text, with no enumerated check, because an unrecognised type must still be stored and flagged [AC-001-04-02]; the domain model names database, compute and backup storage |\n| `region` | text | NULL | none | null when metadata is missing or unreadable (`ASM-BE-554`) |\n| `compliance_status` | text | NOT NULL | `'unverified'` | `CHECK (compliance_status IN ('pass','fail','unverified'))` [backend.domain-model] |\n| `last_scanned_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | null until the first scan |\n| `created_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n\nTable check: `CHECK (compliance_status <> 'pass' OR region IS NOT NULL)`. An unknown or unreadable region can never be recorded as pass (`INV-BE-167`).\n\nIndexes:\n- unique btree `(external_ref)` serves upsert of scan results.\n- btree `(compliance_status)` serves the pass count behind the report and the gate.\n\n**residency_scans** (new, `ASM-BE-557`)\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `scan_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | primary key |\n| `trigger` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (trigger IN ('api','schedule'))` |\n| `status` | text | NOT NULL | `'running'` | `CHECK (status IN ('running','completed','failed'))` |\n| `started_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `finished_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | `CHECK (finished_at IS NULL OR finished_at >= started_at)` |\n| `resources_seen` | integer | NULL | none | `CHECK (resources_seen IS NULL OR resources_seen >= 0)`; set at completion |\n\nIndex: btree `(started_at DESC)` serves the latest-scan lookup.\n\n**drift_alerts** (DRIFT_ALERT)\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `alert_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | primary key |\n| `resource_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | foreign key to `infra_resources`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT` (alerts are compliance evidence) |\n| `scan_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | foreign key to `residency_scans`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT` |\n| `detected_region` | text | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `detected_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | when the scan found the resource outside the approved regions |\n| `raised_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (raised_at >= detected_at)` |\n| `cleared_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | set when a later scan finds the resource in an approved region (`ASM-BE-555`) |\n\nIndexes:\n- unique partial btree `(resource_id, detected_region) WHERE cleared_at IS NULL` enforces one open alert per resource and region (`ASM-BE-555`).\n- btree `(raised_at DESC)` serves the alert listing.\n\n**Time.** Every instant is `timestamptz`.\n\n**PII and secrets.** These tables hold no personal data. Credentials the scanner needs to read the hosting platform are never stored in these tables or in this spec (`GAP-BE-767`).\n\n**Encryption at rest.** No input states it, so none is claimed (`GAP-BE-304`).\n\n**Retention.** Not stated for scan results, alerts or reports (`GAP-BE-305`). None is claimed. Hard deletion of `infra_resources` rows is also unstated (`GAP-BE-774`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\n### 3.1 Resource compliance status (`infra_resources.compliance_status`)\n\nStates: `unverified` (initial), `pass`, `fail`. None is terminal, because every scan re-evaluates every resource. [backend.domain-model]\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `unverified` | resource first inventoried | | row created |\n| any | `pass` | scan evaluates the resource | resource type recognised and `region` is in `approved_regions` | `last_scanned_at` set; any open alert for the resource is cleared (`ASM-BE-555`) |\n| any | `fail` | scan evaluates the resource | resource type recognised and `region` is present and not in `approved_regions` | `last_scanned_at` set; a drift alert is raised unless one is already open for this resource and region (`ASM-BE-555`) |\n| any | `unverified` | scan evaluates the resource | type not recognised, or `region` missing or unreadable (`ASM-BE-554`) | `last_scanned_at` set; `region` kept as read, null if unreadable |\n\nAn `unverified` resource is not EU-verified for the report or the gate (`ASM-BE-552`). An unrecognised type is flagged, never silently omitted. [AC-001-04-02] A resource whose region cannot be read is never treated as a pass. [AC-001-03-03; `INV-BE-007`]\n\n### 3.2 Scan run (`residency_scans.status`)\n\nDerived, not specified by any input (`ASM-BE-557`). States: `running` (initial), `completed` (terminal), `failed` (terminal).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `running` | scan trigger (API or schedule) | | row created |\n| `running` | `completed` | all resources evaluated | | `finished_at` and `resources_seen` set |\n| `running` | `failed` | the scanner cannot read the hosting platform | | `finished_at` set; previous resource results untouched; a failed scan is never read as a pass |\n\nWhether a second trigger is accepted while a scan is `running` is `GAP-BE-775`.\n\n### 3.3 Drift alert\n\nAn alert is open while `cleared_at` is null and cleared when it is set (`ASM-BE-555`). No input defines an acknowledgement or closure workflow (`GAP-BE-771`), so none is specified. There is no terminal state beyond cleared.\n\n### 3.4 Launch gate (derived, not stored)\n\nStates: `PASSED` and `FAILED`, computed from the current report on each request. [AC-001-05-01, AC-001-05-02]\n\n| Condition | Gate |\n|---|---|\n| at least one resource, and every resource has status `pass` (100% EU-verified) | `PASSED` |\n| fewer than 100% `pass`, including any `unverified` resource | `FAILED` |\n| no resources inventoried (`ASM-BE-559`) | `FAILED` |\n\n`PASSED` does not assert that release is authorised (`ASM-BE-560`; `CON-BE-151`, `Q-BE-152`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-001 Validate an EU region\n**Given** a validation request naming a resource and a region that is in `approved_regions`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` is called,\n**Then** the result states that the check passed, so the deployment or provisioning may proceed.
