Nothing is written (`ASM-BE-556`).\n- **Traces to.** US-001-01, US-001-03; AC-001-01-01, AC-001-03-01; BR-001, BR-002.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-validate-eu-pass` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210`.\n- **Status.** Blocked on the approved region list (`Q-BE-151`). Success status and body shape are `GAP-BE-769`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-002 Report a non-EU region as a violation\n**Given** a validation request whose region is not in `approved_regions`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` is called,\n**Then** the result states a residency violation in the response body, naming the offending resource and region. The pipeline fails the build on it. No resource is created by this module.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-01, US-001-03; AC-001-01-02, AC-001-03-02; BR-001, BR-002; NFR-002.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-validate-non-eu-violation` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210`. A violation is reported in the body, not as a pipeline status. [operation record]\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-003 Missing or unreadable region is a violation\n**Given** a validation request where the region is absent, empty or cannot be read,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` is called,\n**Then** the result states a violation, never a pass by default.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-03; AC-001-03-03; `INV-BE-007`.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-validate-missing-region-violation` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-004 Validate a backup destination within 30 seconds\n**Given** a backup destination configuration pointing outside the approved regions,\n**When** the pipeline calls `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` before the backup runs,\n**Then** the result states a residency violation within 30 seconds, so the pipeline can fail the job before any data is written. A destination in an approved region passes.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-02; AC-001-02-01, AC-001-02-02; BR-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-validate-backup-destination-30s` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210`.\n- **Status.** The 30 seconds is the end-to-end budget of the job failing, as written in the AC. Whether this call alone must complete inside it is `GAP-BE-776`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-005 Reject a malformed validation request\n**Given** a validation request missing the resource or region field, or with a field of the wrong type,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` is called,\n**Then** a 400 validation error is returned in the global envelope, distinct from a violation result.\n- **Traces to.** Derived from the status policy in `backend.error-model` (400 for validation failure) [US-004-03]; request shape is `GAP-BE-035`.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-validate-bad-request-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210`.\n- **Distinction.** Missing region metadata inside an otherwise well-formed request is FR-003, a violation. A request that lacks the fields altogether is this requirement.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-006 Scan provisioned infrastructure\n**Given** a scan trigger,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` is called,\n**Then** a `residency_scans` row is created, every inventoried resource is evaluated per state machine 3.1, `region`, `compliance_status` and `last_scanned_at` are recorded, and the scan finishes `completed` or `failed`.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04, US-001-06; AC-001-04-01, AC-001-06-01; NFR-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-scan-records-status` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`.\n- **Status.** How resources are discovered is `GAP-BE-767`. Sync or async response and success status are `GAP-BE-769`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-007 Flag an unrecognised resource type\n**Given** a resource whose type the scanner does not recognise,\n**When** a scan completes and the inventory or report is read,\n**Then** the resource appears with status `unverified` and is counted as not EU-verified.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; AC-001-04-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-unrecognised-type-unverified` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-008 List inventoried resources\n**Given** inventoried resources,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444` is called,\n**Then** each resource is listed with its region, compliance status and last scan time. A never-scanned resource shows `unverified` and no scan time.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; AC-001-04-01, AC-001-04-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-list-resources` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444`.\n- **Status.** Pagination, sorting and filtering are not supplied (`GAP-BE-500` covers page sizes only for search); the collection is unpaginated by default (`GAP-BE-770`).\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-009 Compliance report\n**Given** the current inventory,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798` is called,\n**Then** the response lists each resource, its region and a pass/fail residency status, with an overall % compliant metric. The metric is `pass` resources divided by all resources, so `unverified` resources count against it (`ASM-BE-552`).\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; AC-001-04-01; NFR-001, NFR-004.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-report-percent-compliant` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798`.\n- **Status.** Report fields beyond those named and format are `GAP-BE-770`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-010 Report is refreshed per request\n**Given** any request for the report,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798` is called,\n**Then** the report is recomputed from the current stored results at that request, not served from a cached earlier report, and states the time it was generated and each resource's last scan time (`ASM-BE-553`).\n- **Traces to.** NFR-004 (\"refreshed and 100% accurate at each launch gate check\").\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-report-recomputed-per-request` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798`.\n- **Status.** Whether a request must also trigger a fresh scan, and how old a result may be, is `GAP-BE-773`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-011 Launch gate passes\n**Given** at least one resource and a report with 100% EU-verified resources,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` is called,\n**Then** the gate is reported `PASSED`.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-05; AC-001-05-01; BR-003.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-gate-pass` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`.\n- **Status.** The consequence of `PASSED` for release is blocked on `CON-BE-151` and `Q-BE-152`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-012 Launch gate fails\n**Given** a report with less than 100% EU-verified resources, including any `unverified` resource, or an empty inventory,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` is called,\n**Then** the gate is reported `FAILED`, with the resources that are not EU-verified named, until the report reaches 100%.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-05; AC-001-05-02; BR-003; `ASM-BE-552`, `ASM-BE-559`.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-gate-fail-below-100`, `test-residency-gate-fail-empty-inventory` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-013 Gate is read-only\n**Given** any call to `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`,\n**When** it is evaluated,\n**Then** no row is written, and no sign-off or override is possible through this module.\n- **Traces to.** Operation record for `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`; `CON-BE-151`.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-gate-no-writes` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-014 Raise a drift alert\n**Given** a scan finds a running resource in a region not in `approved_regions`,\n**When** the scan evaluates it,\n**Then** a drift alert is raised carrying the resource details (resource, type, detected region, time), no later than 15 minutes after detection, and the resource is set to `fail`.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-02; BR-004; NFR-003.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-drift-alert-raised` (planned, injectable clock).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`.\n- **Status.** The AC says \"immediately\" and NFR-003 says within 15 minutes: `CON-BE-451`. Delivery to the DevOps team is `GAP-BE-308`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-015 No alert on a clean scan\n**Given** a periodic scan in which every resource remains in an approved region,\n**When** the scan completes,\n**Then** no alert is raised and the scan logs a pass.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-01.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-scan-clean-no-alert` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-016 No duplicate alerts\n**Given** an open alert for a resource and region,\n**When** a later scan finds the same resource still in that region,\n**Then** no second alert is created.\n- **Traces to.** `ASM-BE-555` (not stated by any input).\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-drift-alert-dedup` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-017 List drift alerts\n**Given** raised drift alerts,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` is called,\n**Then** the alerts are listed with resource, detected region, detection time and raised time.\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-02; NFR-003.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-list-drift-alerts` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-018 A failed scan is never a pass\n**Given** a scan that cannot read the hosting platform,\n**When** it ends `failed`,\n**Then** earlier results are not overwritten and the failure is visible. It is not logged as a pass.\n- **Traces to.** Derived from AC-001-03-03 (fail rather than pass by default) and AC-001-06-01 (a pass is logged only for a scan that runs).\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-scan-failure-not-pass` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` | GET | `/residency/drift-alerts` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403 | List drift alerts raised when a resource is detected outside approved EU regions, to be raised within 15 minutes of detection (NFR-003). Alert channel and recipients are open (GAP-BE-308). Proposed path (GAP-BE-303). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` | GET | `/residency/launch-gate` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 401, 403, 503 | Evaluate the launch gate from the current compliance report. Read-only. Whether a pass authorises release or Engineering Lead sign-off is required is unresolved (CON-BE-151, Q-BE-152), so no sign-off operation is defined. Proposed path (GAP-BE-303). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798` | GET | `/residency/report` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 401, 403, 503 | Return the residency compliance report covering all infrastructure components, refreshed at each request so it is accurate at launch gate checks (NFR-004). Proposed path (GAP-BE-303); storage of report data is open (GAP-BE-305). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444` | GET | `/residency/resources` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403 | List inventoried infrastructure resources with region, compliance status and last scan time. Resource types the scanner does not recognise appear with status unverified (AC-001-04-02). Proposed path (GAP-BE-303). |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` | POST | `/residency/scans` | `residency-compliance` | 202 | 400, 401, 403, 404, 409, 503 | Trigger a residency scan of provisioned infrastructure, recording region and compliance status per resource. Proposed path (GAP-BE-303). Drift alerts are raised for any resource found outside approved regions. |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` | POST | `/residency/validations` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 503 | Validate a resource's declared region against the approved EU region list (CI/CD residency gate and provisioning check). Proposed path (GAP-BE-303); approved region list pending (Q-BE-151). A violation is reported in the body, as pipeline failure handling is outside HTTP. |\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` \u2014 GET `/residency/drift-alerts`\n\nList drift alerts raised when a resource is detected outside approved EU regions, to be raised within 15 minutes of detection (NFR-003). Alert channel and recipients are open (GAP-BE-308). Proposed path (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** \n- `resource_id` (string): Query filter by resource.\n- `raised_after` (string): Query filter: only alerts raised after this timestamp.\n- `page` (integer): Page number.\n- `page_size` (integer): Items per page.\n**Response:** \n- `items` (array, required): Drift alerts, each with id, resource_id, detected_region and raised_at.\n- `total` (integer, required): Total number of matching alerts.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` \u2014 GET `/residency/launch-gate`\n\nEvaluate the launch gate from the current compliance report. Read-only. Whether a pass authorises release or Engineering Lead sign-off is required is unresolved (CON-BE-151, Q-BE-152), so no sign-off operation is defined. Proposed path (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `gate_passed` (boolean, required): True only when every component is compliant.\n- `evaluated_at` (string, required): Timestamp of the evaluation against a freshly refreshed report.\n- `blocking_resources` (array, required): Resources that are failing or unverified and prevent the gate passing; empty when passed.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798` \u2014 GET `/residency/report`\n\nReturn the residency compliance report covering all infrastructure components, refreshed at each request so it is accurate at launch gate checks (NFR-004). Proposed path (GAP-BE-303); storage of report data is open (GAP-BE-305).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `generated_at` (string, required): Timestamp at which the report was generated.\n- `total_components` (integer, required): Number of infrastructure components assessed.\n- `verified_eu_components` (integer, required): Number of components verified as being in approved EU regions.\n- `non_compliant_components` (integer, required): Number of components in the fail state.\n- `unverified_components` (integer, required): Number of components flagged unverified, including unrecognised types.\n- `components` (array, required): Per-component entries with resource_id, resource_type, region, compliance_status and last_scanned_at.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444` \u2014 GET `/residency/resources`\n\nList inventoried infrastructure resources with region, compliance status and last scan time. Resource types the scanner does not recognise appear with status unverified (AC-001-04-02). Proposed path (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** \n- `compliance_status` (string): Query filter: pass, fail or unverified.\n- `resource_type` (string): Query filter: database, compute or backup storage.\n- `page` (integer): Page number; default and maximum page size are not stated (GAP-BE-500).\n- `page_size` (integer): Items per page.\n**Response:** \n- `items` (array, required): Resources, each with resource_id, resource_type, region, compliance_status and last_scanned_at.\n- `total` (integer, required): Total number of matching resources.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` \u2014 POST `/residency/scans`\n\nTrigger a residency scan of provisioned infrastructure, recording region and compliance status per resource. Proposed path (GAP-BE-303). Drift alerts are raised for any resource found outside approved regions.\n\n**Request:** \n- `resource_ids` (array): Optional subset of resource identifiers to scan; all resources are scanned when omitted.\n**Response:** \n- `scan_started_at` (string, required): Timestamp at which the scan was accepted.\n- `resources_queued` (integer, required): Number of resources included in the scan.\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` \u2014 POST `/residency/validations`\n\nValidate a resource's declared region against the approved EU region list (CI/CD residency gate and provisioning check). Proposed path (GAP-BE-303); approved region list pending (Q-BE-151). A violation is reported in the body, as pipeline failure handling is outside HTTP.\n\n**Request:** \n- `resource_id` (string, required): Identifier of the infrastructure component being checked.\n- `resource_type` (string, required): Component type: database, compute or backup storage. Unrecognised types are flagged unverified, not rejected.\n- `region` (string): Declared region of the resource. If missing or unreadable the check fails and deployment is blocked (AC-001-03-03).\n**Response:** \n- `resource_id` (string, required): Identifier of the checked resource.\n- `region` (string): Region as read for the resource; absent when metadata is missing or unreadable.\n- `compliance_status` (string, required): Result: pass, fail or unverified.\n- `deployment_blocked` (boolean, required): True when the check fails, including missing or unreadable region metadata.\n- `violation_reason` (string): Residency violation reason present when status is fail.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\nThese hold after every operation. `INV-BE-007` (a residency check with missing or unreadable region metadata fails and blocks deployment) is defined in the project spec and applies here by reference.\n\n- `INV-BE-160`. A validation never reports a pass for a region that is missing, unreadable or not in `approved_regions`. [AC-001-01-02, AC-001-03-02, AC-001-03-03]\n- `INV-BE-161`. A validation request writes nothing (`ASM-BE-556`).\n- `INV-BE-162`. A resource has status `pass` only if its last-scanned region is in `approved_regions`.\n- `INV-BE-163`. Every inventoried resource appears in the inventory and in the report. A resource of an unrecognised type appears as `unverified` and is never omitted. [AC-001-04-02]\n- `INV-BE-164`. The % compliant metric equals `pass` resources divided by all inventoried resources (`ASM-BE-552`). The gate is `PASSED` only when that is 100% and at least one resource exists (`ASM-BE-559`). [AC-001-05-01, AC-001-05-02]\n- `INV-BE-165`. For every resource with status `fail` and a detected region, an alert exists that is open (`cleared_at` null) for that resource and region. No resource with status `pass` has an open alert. (`ASM-BE-555`)\n- `INV-BE-166`. At most one open alert exists per resource and region. `raised_at` is never earlier than `detected_at`. A clean scan raises no alert. [AC-001-06-01]\n- `INV-BE-167`. A row with `compliance_status` `pass` has a non-null `region` (enforced by table check).\n- `INV-BE-168`. The gate and report endpoints change no state (`API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-894798`, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-381444` and `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` are reads).\n- `INV-BE-169`. A scan with status `failed` changes no resource status and no alert.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## 7. Authorisation\n\n**Blocked.** The permission matrix (role \u00d7 operation) cannot be written.\n\n- **Roles.** No input defines API caller roles. The existing authentication method is unnamed (`GAP-BE-085`), and the project authorisation model is itself `BLOCKED` (`backend.auth-model`).\n- **Intent the stories state.** These are the actors named by the stories, not API roles. Each wants these operations:\n  - Platform/DevOps Engineer: scans and drift alerts. [US-001-01, US-001-02, US-001-06]\n  - Release/CI Pipeline Owner: the validation check. [US-001-03]\n  - Compliance/Security Auditor: the report and inventory. [US-001-04]\n  - Engineering Lead: the gate. [US-001-05]\n- **Machine callers.** The pipeline calls `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-405210` without a person. How a machine caller is identified is not stated (`GAP-BE-772`).\n- **Ownership predicate.** None applies. Resources are infrastructure, not user-owned. Nothing in this module is scoped to a user.\n- **Default.** A role has no permission on an operation until an input grants it (`ASM-BE-302`).\n- **Sign-off.** No operation grants release sign-off (`CON-BE-151`, `Q-BE-152`).\n\n**Unblocked by:**\n- a statement of the existing authentication method and its roles (`GAP-BE-085`);\n- a decision on which roles may call each of the six operations and how the pipeline is identified (`GAP-BE-772`);\n- the resolution of `CON-BE-151` and `Q-BE-152` for the gate.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n### 8.1 Periodic residency scan\n\n- **Purpose.** After launch, detect any running resource that has drifted to or is found in a non-EU region, for example through misconfigured auto-scaling. [US-001-06; AC-001-06-02; BR-004]\n- **Schedule.** The period is not stated (`Q-BE-153`). The period is no longer than 15 minutes, because the alert must be fired within 15 minutes of detection (`ASM-BE-153`, NFR-003).\n- **Action.** The same scan as `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`, with `trigger = 'schedule'`.\n- **Output.** A clean scan logs a pass and raises nothing. [AC-001-06-01] A finding raises an alert per BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-014. A failed scan is recorded and never read as a pass (FR-018).\n- **Overlap.** A schedule tick while a scan is `running` is skipped (`ASM-BE-562`). The same rule for the API trigger is `GAP-BE-775`.\n- **Retry.** No retry policy is stated. A failed scan waits for the next tick (`GAP-BE-775`).\n\n### 8.2 Alert clearing\n\nClearing is part of the scan, not a separate job (`ASM-BE-555`). A scan that finds a previously alerting resource in an approved region sets `cleared_at`.\n\n### 8.3 Idempotency\n\n- Resource upsert is keyed on `external_ref`. Re-running a scan over unchanged resources produces the same resource rows and no new alert (FR-016).\n- Each scan run has its own `scan_id`. Scan runs are not deduplicated: two triggers produce two runs (overlap rule above).\n- The validation operation is stateless and idempotent by construction (`ASM-BE-556`).\n\n### 8.4 Retention\n\nNo retention job is specified. NFR-019 covers login records only, and retention of scan results, alerts and reports is not stated (`GAP-BE-305`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\nAll names are planned. Tests that depend on the region list use a fixture list, because the real list is `Q-BE-151`. The injectable clock is required for every time-based test.\n\n### Unit\n- `test-residency-region-match-exact` \u2014 comparison against `approved_regions` (`ASM-BE-561`).\n- `test-residency-status-transitions` \u2014 table 3.1, every row including the unrecognised-type and missing-region rows.\n- `test-residency-percent-compliant-math` \u2014 `pass` divided by all, with `unverified` in the denominator (`ASM-BE-552`).\n- `test-residency-gate-evaluation` \u2014 100%, below 100%, empty inventory (`ASM-BE-559`).\n- `test-residency-alert-dedup-key` \u2014 one open alert per resource and region.\n- `test-residency-percent-nan-guard` \u2014 empty inventory gives a defined result, not a division error.\n\n### Integration\n- `test-residency-validate-eu-pass`, `test-residency-validate-non-eu-violation`, `test-residency-validate-missing-region-violation`, `test-residency-validate-bad-request-400`.\n- `test-residency-validate-backup-destination-30s`.\n- `test-residency-scan-records-status`, `test-residency-unrecognised-type-unverified`, `test-residency-scan-failure-not-pass`.\n- `test-residency-list-resources`, `test-residency-report-percent-compliant`, `test-residency-report-recomputed-per-request`.\n- `test-residency-gate-pass`, `test-residency-gate-fail-below-100`, `test-residency-gate-fail-empty-inventory`, `test-residency-gate-no-writes`.\n- `test-residency-drift-alert-raised`, `test-residency-scan-clean-no-alert`, `test-residency-drift-alert-dedup`, `test-residency-list-drift-alerts`.\n- `test-residency-alert-cleared-on-return-to-eu` (`ASM-BE-555`).\n- `test-residency-scheduled-scan-skips-overlap` (`ASM-BE-562`).\n- `test-residency-db-check-pass-requires-region` \u2014 the table check from the data model.\n\n### Contract\n- Every operation in scope appears in the published OpenAPI document with request and response schemas and its 4xx and 5xx errors. [BR-012; NFR-008] Run by `api-docs`; it needs the success statuses (`GAP-BE-769`).\n- Error responses use the global envelope. [backend.error-model]\n- A violation result and a 400 validation error are distinguishable in the documented schemas (FR-005).\n- The data-handling note in the OpenAPI document states EU residency of note data. [BR-013; US-004-06] The note is owned by `api-docs`; this module supplies the region list source.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.gaps | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\nOpen items defined in the project spec are cited, not redefined: `Q-BE-151`, `Q-BE-152`, `Q-BE-153`, `CON-BE-151`, `GAP-BE-035`, `GAP-BE-085`, `GAP-BE-304`, `GAP-BE-305`, `GAP-BE-307`, `GAP-BE-308`, `GAP-BE-500`, `GAP-BE-602`, `ASM-BE-153`, `ASM-BE-302`. Items below are new to this module.\n\n### Contradictions\n\n- `CON-BE-451` (non-critical). AC-001-06-02 says an alert is sent \"immediately\" when a resource is found in a non-EU region. NFR-003 says the alert is fired \"within 15 minutes of drift detection\". Reading used: the alert is raised in the same scan run that detects the drift, and in any case within 15 minutes of detection. Consequence of the AC reading: no latency budget can be tested beyond \"same run\". Consequence of the NFR reading: a 15-minute budget is the only testable bound.\n\n### Gaps\n\n| ID | Missing | Degrades | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-767` | How the scanner discovers resources, which resource types it recognises, and how it is authorised to read the hosting platform. No input names a mechanism. No credential is written here. | FR-006, FR-007, scan job | a statement of the inventory source and the recognised-type list |\n| `GAP-BE-768` | The format of region identifiers and whether the list is maintained in configuration or data. `Q-BE-151` supplies the values. | FR-001 to FR-004, `approved_regions` | the approved region list with its source |\n| `GAP-BE-769` | Success status and body of the six operations, and whether a scan is synchronous or returns a run to poll. | api-contract, contract tests | a decision on statuses and the scan response |\n| `GAP-BE-770` | Report and inventory shape beyond the fields the ACs name: format, pagination, sorting, filtering, history of earlier reports kept for audit. | FR-008, FR-009 | a report specification |\n| `GAP-BE-771` | Alert lifecycle: acknowledgement, who closes an alert, repeat notification while a resource stays out of region. | state machine 3.3, FR-016 | an alert handling rule |\n| `GAP-BE-772` | How a machine caller such as the CI pipeline is identified, and which roles may call each operation. | authorization | an authentication decision (`GAP-BE-085`) |\n| `GAP-BE-773` | Maximum age of a scan result for the report and the gate, and whether a gate or report request triggers a fresh scan. NFR-004 requires accuracy at each gate check. | FR-010, FR-011, FR-012 | a freshness rule |\n| `GAP-BE-774` | What happens when a resource is decommissioned: removal from inventory, deletion of its alerts. | data-model | a decommissioning rule |\n| `GAP-BE-775` | Behaviour of an API scan trigger while a scan is running, and the retry rule for a failed scan. | jobs 8.1 | an operations decision |\n| `GAP-BE-776` | Which part of the 30-second budget this service owns in AC-001-02-02, and whether validations are logged for audit. | FR-004 | a latency allocation and an audit decision |\n\nNo latency or throughput target is supplied for the scan, report or gate operations, and none is invented (`GAP-BE-301` covers login only).\n\n### Assumptions\n\n- `ASM-BE-551`. Column types: uuid keys, text strings, timestamptz instants, integer counts. PostgreSQL 16 is the platform and no types are supplied. Confirmed by a platform decision.\n- `ASM-BE-552`. An `unverified` resource is not EU-verified. The % compliant metric is `pass` divided by all resources. AC-001-04-02 flags `unverified`, and AC-001-05-02 blocks at less than 100% EU-verified. Confirmed by a product-owner decision.\n- `ASM-BE-553`. \"Refreshed at each request\" means recomputed from the latest stored scan results, with each row's last scan time shown, not that a request triggers a scan. NFR-004 does not say. Confirmed by a decision on `GAP-BE-773`.\n- `ASM-BE-554`. A scan whose resource has a missing or unreadable region records status `unverified` with a null region; the validation operation reports a violation for the same case. AC-001-03-03 requires a failure for validation and says nothing about inventory status. Confirmed by a product-owner decision.\n- `ASM-BE-555`. One open alert exists per resource and region. A later scan that finds the resource in an approved region sets `cleared_at`. A repeat scan with the resource still out of region raises no second alert. No input defines the alert lifecycle (`GAP-BE-771`). Confirmed by an alert handling rule.\n- `ASM-BE-556`. The validation operation is stateless and writes nothing. The operation record describes a check, and no input asks for validation history. Confirmed by a decision on `GAP-BE-776`.\n- `ASM-BE-557`. A `residency_scans` record is needed to run, order and audit scans. It extends the project domain model, which lists only INFRA_RESOURCE and DRIFT_ALERT. Confirmed by a design review.\n- `ASM-BE-559`. An empty inventory fails the gate. 100% of nothing cannot be shown as EU-verified. Confirmed by a product-owner decision.\n- `ASM-BE-560`. The gate endpoint returns the computed outcome only. It does not assert release authorisation, because `CON-BE-151` is open. Confirmed by the resolution of `CON-BE-151`.\n- `ASM-BE-561`. Region comparison is exact and case-sensitive against `approved_regions`, with no normalisation. Confirmed by `GAP-BE-768`.\n- `ASM-BE-562`. A schedule tick while a scan is `running` is skipped. Confirmed by a decision on `GAP-BE-775`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-001-01", "US-001-02", "US-001-03", "US-001-04", "US-001-05", "US-001-06"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/api-docs-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/api-docs-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: api-docs\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [Q-BE-005, CON-BE-006, Q-BE-151]\nscope: [API-APIDOCS-098363]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The `api-docs` module owns the published OpenAPI document for notes_api and the checks that keep it complete and accurate. [backend.module-map; stories.md US-004-01, US-004-02, US-004-04, US-004-05, US-004-06, US-005-01]\n\n1. **Serve the document.** `API-APIDOCS-098363` returns an OpenAPI 3.x document that lists all CRUD, tagging, search, archive and email one-time-code login (send and verify) endpoints. [US-004-01]\n2. **Completeness.** Every implemented endpoint has an entry with request and response schemas and its 4xx/5xx errors. [BR-012, NFR-008]\n3. **Schema documentation.** Required fields, types and constraints are documented, and optional parameters are marked optional with their defaults. [US-004-02]\n4. **Spec-versus-code integrity.** A documented field type that differs from the code fails a validation check. [US-004-02]\n5. **Coverage metric.** The module computes the share of implemented endpoints present in the spec and names any that are missing. [US-004-04]\n6. **Examples.** Search and tagging operations carry example requests and responses, including an empty-result example. [US-004-05, NFR-009]\n7. **Data-handling note.** The document states where active and archived note data is stored and served, and calls out any difference between them. [US-004-06, BR-013]\n8. **Scope section.** The document lists mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration as explicitly excluded. [US-005-01]\n9. **Interface-change documentation.** The document describes the `pinned` field and the pinned-first ordering [NFR-014], and the email one-time-code endpoints as an additive change [NFR-016].\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- Defining any endpoint, schema, status code or error code. These belong to the module that owns the operation and to `backend.error-model`. This module documents them by reading those records and restates none (core rule 6).\n- Minting `API-` or `SCHEMA-` identifiers. DevAgent mints them.\n- The scope-guardrail contract test suite and runtime rejection of out-of-scope calls (`scope-guardrail`). It reads this module's output. [module-map]\n- Residency enforcement, scanning, the compliance report and the launch gate (`residency-compliance`). This module only states residency facts supplied by inputs.\n- Notifying consumers of interface changes (`GAP-BE-822`).\n- Logging or classifying stakeholder requests. [BR-017]\n- Any UI.\n\n**Operation owned.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`, referenced by id only.\n\n**Shared story.** US-004-03 (documented 4xx/5xx for each endpoint) is mapped to `api-docs`, `auth` and `error-model` in `backend.coverage`. This module requires that errors be documented (BE-API-DOCS-FR-006). It defines no status.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\n**No database tables.** No input says the document or the coverage result is persisted in PostgreSQL. This module appears in no migration of `backend.migration-plan`. The document is treated as a generated build artefact (`ASM-BE-601`).\n\nThe logical artefacts below are not tables. The coverage report is a build result. Where it is stored and how it is exposed are `GAP-BE-817`.\n\n**Published OpenAPI document (artefact)**\n\n| Part | Content | Nullability | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| OpenAPI version | a 3.x version | required | US-004-01 |\n| Paths | one entry per implemented endpoint | required, non-empty | BR-012 |\n| Schemas | request and response shapes, with required fields, types and constraints | required per endpoint | US-004-02 |\n| Error responses | 4xx/5xx per endpoint, referencing `backend.error-model` | required per endpoint | BR-012, US-004-03 |\n| Examples | search and tagging request and response examples, including an empty search result | required for search and tagging | NFR-009, US-004-05 |\n| Data-handling note | where active and archived note data is stored and served | required | BR-013, US-004-06 |\n| Scope section | the exclusions: mobile apps, attachments, real-time collaboration | required | US-005-01 |\n| Interface-change notes | `pinned` field and ordering; additive login endpoints | required | NFR-014, NFR-016 |\n\n**Coverage report (build result)**\n\n| Field | Meaning | Constraint |\n|---|---|---|\n| implemented endpoint count | endpoints in the route registry | integer, at least 0 |\n| documented endpoint count | those present in the spec | integer, at most the implemented count |\n| coverage percent | documented over implemented | 100 only when no implemented endpoint is missing and the spec parsed |\n| missing endpoints | names of implemented endpoints absent from the spec | empty when coverage is 100 |\n| outcome | `COVERED`, `INCOMPLETE` or `PARSE_ERROR` | see `backend.api-docs.state-machine` |\n| example coverage | share of search and tagging endpoints with example payloads | target 100% [NFR-009] |\n\n**Constraints.**\n\n- Every example is masked and contains no real-looking passport number, date of birth, phone number or email address, and no credential. [core rule 12]\n- Field-level constraints such as title length and tag format cannot be documented until they exist (`GAP-BE-035`).\n- Page size defaults and maximum are `GAP-BE-500`.\n\n**Indexes.** None.\n\n**PII and encryption.** The artefact stores no personal data. No encryption claim is made (`GAP-BE-304`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\nThe entity is a **documentation build**. It is a derived lifecycle, because the inputs state outcomes but name no states (`ASM-BE-601`, `ASM-BE-605`).\n\nStates: `GENERATED`, `VALIDATING`, `REJECTED` (terminal), `PUBLISHED` (terminal for that build).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `GENERATED` | build runs | none | document built from the route registry and operation records |\n| `GENERATED` | `VALIDATING` | checks start | none | checks run (`ASM-BE-605`) |\n| `VALIDATING` | `REJECTED` | spec does not parse | none | fails with a clear parsing error; no coverage figure is reported [US-004-04] |\n| `VALIDATING` | `REJECTED` | Scope section missing | none | fails with a lint/validation error [US-005-01] |\n| `VALIDATING` | `REJECTED` | documented field type differs from code | none | CI check fails and flags the mismatch [US-004-02] |\n| `VALIDATING` | `REJECTED` | coverage below 100% | `ASM-BE-606` | the missing endpoint is named [US-004-04] |\n| `VALIDATING` | `PUBLISHED` | all checks pass | coverage 100%, parses, Scope section present, no type mismatch | document becomes the one served by `API-APIDOCS-098363` |\n\n- `REJECTED` and `PUBLISHED` are terminal for a build. The next build starts at `GENERATED`.\n- The document served is the latest `PUBLISHED` one.\n- A new endpoint must appear in the published document before or at the same time it becomes callable. [US-004-01, `INV-BE-210`]\n- What happens when no document has ever been published is `GAP-BE-824`.\n- An illegal transition has no HTTP response, because a build is not a request.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-001 Serve the OpenAPI document\n**Given** a deployed notes_api,\n**When** a client calls `API-APIDOCS-098363`,\n**Then** a valid OpenAPI 3.x document is returned with status 200, listing all CRUD, tagging, search, archive, and email one-time-code login (send and verify) endpoints, and itself.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01; BR-012; NFR-008; `backend.error-model` (200 for a successful read).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-serve-valid-document` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** Format and content type are `ASM-BE-604` and `GAP-BE-819`. Authentication is `GAP-BE-820`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-002 Unsupported Accept header\n**Given** a request with an unsupported Accept header,\n**When** `API-APIDOCS-098363` is called,\n**Then** the response is 406 with the error code `NOT_ACCEPTABLE` (`ASM-BE-603`), or a default-format fallback with a clear content type.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01; `backend.error-model`.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-accept-unsupported` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** The choice between 406 and fallback is `GAP-BE-309`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-003 New endpoint appears no later than it is callable\n**Given** a new endpoint added to notes_api,\n**When** the spec is regenerated and published,\n**Then** the endpoint appears in the document before or at the same time it becomes callable.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01; `INV-BE-210`.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-new-endpoint-listed` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** Generation method is `GAP-BE-818`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-004 Document schemas and constraints\n**Given** an endpoint that accepts a note payload,\n**When** its schema is inspected in the spec,\n**Then** all required fields, types and constraints are documented.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02; BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-note-schema-complete` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** Title length and tag format are `GAP-BE-035`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-005 Document optional parameters\n**Given** an endpoint with optional query parameters,\n**When** its schema is reviewed,\n**Then** each is marked optional and its default is documented. For search, `include_archived` defaults to excluding archived notes.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02; BR-009.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-optional-params-defaults` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** Page size defaults are `GAP-BE-500`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-006 Document errors per endpoint\n**Given** any implemented endpoint,\n**When** the spec is reviewed,\n**Then** it carries the 4xx/5xx responses that the owning module and `backend.error-model` define for it, each with its error structure.\n- This module restates no status and defines no error code.\n- **Traces to.** BR-012; NFR-008; US-004-03 (shared).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-errors-match-records` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-007 Detect spec-versus-code drift\n**Given** a documented field type is changed in code and the spec is not updated,\n**When** the spec-validation CI check runs,\n**Then** it fails and flags the mismatch.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-type-drift-fails` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** Trigger is `ASM-BE-605`. Mechanism is `GAP-BE-818`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-008 Coverage metric\n**Given** the route registry and the published spec,\n**When** the coverage check runs,\n**Then** it reports 100% if every implemented endpoint is documented. Otherwise it reports less than 100% and names each missing endpoint. If the spec is malformed it fails with a parsing error and reports no coverage figure.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04; BR-012; NFR-008.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-coverage-full`, `test-apidocs-coverage-missing-named`, `test-apidocs-coverage-malformed` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** Exposure is `GAP-BE-817`. Effect of less than 100% on publication is `ASM-BE-606`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-009 Examples for search and tagging\n**Given** the search and tagging operations,\n**When** the spec is viewed,\n**Then** each carries an example request (with query parameters for search) and an example response body, and search also carries an example empty-result response. All examples are masked and contain no real-looking PII or credential.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-05; NFR-009.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-examples-present`, `test-apidocs-examples-no-pii` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-010 Data-handling note\n**Given** the published document,\n**When** an operations engineer reviews its data-handling note,\n**Then** it states that all note data is stored and served within the EU, and states any difference in storage location between archive endpoints and active-note endpoints. Under `ASM-BE-602` there is no difference.\n- **Traces to.** US-004-06; BR-013; BR-001; BR-007.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-data-handling-note` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** Naming the approved regions is `BLOCKED` on `Q-BE-151`. The note covers note data only. Whether the email address leaves the EU is `Q-BE-005` and `CON-BE-006`, and the note makes no claim about it.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-011 Scope section\n**Given** the published document,\n**When** a reader views the Scope section,\n**Then** mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration are listed as explicitly excluded.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01; BR-014 to BR-016.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-scope-section-present` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-012 Scope section is build-enforced and change-reviewed\n**Given** a docs build whose output lacks the Scope section,\n**When** the build runs,\n**Then** the build fails with a lint/validation error. **Given** a proposed addition or change to an out-of-scope item, **when** it is submitted, **then** it requires review and approval before merge.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-scope-missing-fails-build` (planned). The approval rule is process, not code.\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** The approval mechanism is `GAP-BE-821`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-013 Interface-change documentation\n**Given** the `pinned` field and pinned-first ordering, and the email one-time-code endpoints,\n**When** the document is published,\n**Then** the `pinned` field and the ordering are documented as an interface change [NFR-014]. The send and verify endpoints are documented as additive, with existing login methods unchanged [NFR-016].\n- **Traces to.** NFR-014; NFR-015; NFR-016.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-interface-change-notes` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n- **Status.** Consumer notification is `GAP-BE-822`.\n\n### Story coverage\n\n| Story | Requirements |\n|---|---|\n| US-004-01 | BE-API-DOCS-FR-001, 002, 003, 013 |\n| US-004-02 | BE-API-DOCS-FR-004, 005, 007 |\n| US-004-04 | BE-API-DOCS-FR-008 |\n| US-004-05 | BE-API-DOCS-FR-009 |\n| US-004-06 | BE-API-DOCS-FR-010 |\n| US-005-01 | BE-API-DOCS-FR-011, 012 |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-APIDOCS-098363` | GET | `/openapi.json` | `api-docs` | 200 | 406 | Fetch the published OpenAPI document for notes_api. The document covers every endpoint, with examples for search and tagging endpoints. It also carries the data-handling note (EU residency of active and archived note data) and the Scope section (attachments, real-time and mobile-specific calls excluded). The path is proposed because no input supplies one (GAP-BE-303). |\n\n### `API-APIDOCS-098363` \u2014 GET `/openapi.json`\n\nFetch the published OpenAPI document for notes_api. The document covers every endpoint, with examples for search and tagging endpoints. It also carries the data-handling note (EU residency of active and archived note data) and the Scope section (attachments, real-time and mobile-specific calls excluded). The path is proposed because no input supplies one (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `openapi` (string, required): OpenAPI specification version the document conforms to.\n- `info` (object, required): API metadata. It carries the title, the version, the data-handling note stating where active and archived note data is stored and served (BR-013, US-004-06), and the Scope section stating the excluded attachment, real-time and mobile-specific calls (BR-014 to BR-016, US-005-01).\n- `paths` (object, required): Every endpoint with parameters, request and response schemas, and the documented error statuses (BR-012, NFR-008). Search and tagging endpoints include examples (NFR-009).\n- `components` (object): Shared schemas, including the error envelope and the error-code registry, referenced from paths.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\n- `INV-BE-210`. After every publication, each endpoint that is callable appears in the published document. A new endpoint is never callable before it is documented. [US-004-01, BR-012]\n- `INV-BE-211`. The coverage check never reports 100% when the spec is malformed or when any implemented endpoint is missing from it. [US-004-04]\n- `INV-BE-212`. A published document always contains the Scope section listing mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration. A build without it is not published. [US-005-01]\n- `INV-BE-213`. No published document carries a field type that differs from the code. A mismatch blocks the build. [US-004-02]\n- `INV-BE-214`. Every search and tagging operation in a published document carries an example payload, and search carries an empty-result example. [NFR-009, US-004-05]\n- `INV-BE-215`. The published document and its examples contain no credential and no real-looking PII. [core rule 12]\n- `INV-BE-216`. `API-APIDOCS-098363` changes no state. The document served is the latest published one. [derived from `backend.api-docs.state-machine`]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\n**Blocked.** Whether `API-APIDOCS-098363` is public or requires a credential is not stated in any input (`GAP-BE-820`). It is an authorisation rule, so it is not defaulted.\n\n- **Role matrix.** No input defines API roles, and `backend.auth-model` is itself `BLOCKED`.\n- **Default.** The default `ASM-BE-302` (no permission unless an input grants one) would deny every caller. That cannot be right for US-004-01, whose integrator fetches the document to learn the API. This is not recorded as a contradiction, because no input says the document is protected.\n- **Ownership predicate.** None applies. The document is not user-scoped and holds no user data (`backend.api-docs.data-model`).\n\n**Unblocked by:** a decision on whether the spec endpoint is public, and the role definitions that `backend.auth-model` waits on.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.jobs | status: UNKNOWN -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\nNo scheduled work, sweeps or retries are specified by any input for this module.\n\n- **Build-time checks.** These run per build, not on a schedule:\n  - spec parse and validation\n  - the type-drift check [US-004-02]\n  - the coverage check [US-004-04]\n  - Scope section lint [US-005-01]\n  - example presence [NFR-009]\n- **Trigger.** Every CI build (`ASM-BE-605`). The cadence is not stated by any input for these checks (`GAP-BE-823`).\n- **Retries.** None specified.\n- **Idempotency.** `API-APIDOCS-098363` is a GET. No idempotency key applies. Each check is a pure function of its inputs.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\n**Unit** (planned)\n- `test-apidocs-coverage-percent-calc`: zero, partial and full coverage, and the empty registry.\n- `test-apidocs-coverage-missing-named`\n- `test-apidocs-scope-lint`: Scope section present, missing, or listing fewer than the three exclusions.\n- `test-apidocs-examples-no-pii`: no real-looking PII or credential in any example.\n\n**Integration** (planned)\n- `test-apidocs-serve-valid-document`: valid OpenAPI 3.x, with all endpoint groups present.\n- `test-apidocs-accept-unsupported`\n- `test-apidocs-new-endpoint-listed`: add an endpoint and assert it is documented no later than it is callable.\n- `test-apidocs-coverage-malformed`: a malformed spec produces a parse error and no coverage figure.\n- `test-apidocs-type-drift-fails`: change a field type in code without updating the spec.\n- `test-apidocs-scope-missing-fails-build`\n\n**Contract** (planned)\n- `test-apidocs-errors-match-records`: documented errors equal the operation records and `backend.error-model`.\n- `test-apidocs-optional-params-defaults`\n- `test-apidocs-data-handling-note`: the note states the EU residency claim and any archive-versus-active difference.\n- `test-apidocs-interface-change-notes`: the `pinned` field, ordering, and the additive login endpoints.\n- Validate the served document with an OpenAPI 3.x validator. The tool is unnamed (`GAP-BE-089`).\n\n**Boundary.** The expiry boundary of login codes is documented elsewhere (`GAP-BE-306`). This module only checks that the document states what the owning module decided.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.gaps | status: VERIFIED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\n**Contradiction check.**\n- Story to design: none found. The brief covers only US-001-01 to US-001-06 and US-002-01.\n- Story to requirement: none new for this module.\n- Requirement to requirement: none new for this module.\n- Design to design: none.\n- No `CON-` is minted by this module. `CON-BE-006` affects only the data-handling note.\n\n**New gaps**\n\n| ID | Gap | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-817` | US-004-04 says to \"compute and expose\" the coverage metric. No operation is minted for it, so exposure is undecided (CI output, build artefact or endpoint). Degrades BE-API-DOCS-FR-008. | A decision on exposure and, if an endpoint, an operation record. |\n| `GAP-BE-818` | No input says how the document is generated from the code or how the route registry is read. This degrades FR-003 and FR-007. | A decision on the generation method and tooling (see `GAP-BE-089`). |\n| `GAP-BE-819` | US-004-01 allows \"JSON/YAML\". The format served at `/openapi.json`, its exact 3.x version and its content type are not stated. | A decision on format, version and content type. |\n| `GAP-BE-820` | Whether `API-APIDOCS-098363` requires authentication is not stated. Section `authorization` is `BLOCKED`. | A decision on public versus protected access. |\n| `GAP-BE-821` | The review/approval mechanism for Scope section changes is not named. | A statement of the review process. |\n| `GAP-BE-822` | How and by whom consumers are notified of interface changes (NFR-014 to NFR-016) is not stated. | A notification owner and channel. |\n| `GAP-BE-823` | The cadence and trigger of the coverage, drift and lint checks are not stated. | A CI pipeline definition. |\n| `GAP-BE-824` | The behaviour of `API-APIDOCS-098363` before any document has been published is not stated. | A decision on that behaviour. |\n\n**Assumptions**\n\n| ID | Assumption | Basis and what would confirm it |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-601` | The document is a generated build artefact built from the route registry and operation records, not hand-authored and not stored in PostgreSQL. | BR-012 refers to a \"route registry\" and an automated coverage check. Confirm by `GAP-BE-818`. |\n| `ASM-BE-602` | Active and archived notes share one store, distinguished by a status flag. The documentation therefore states no storage-location difference between archive and active endpoints. | BR-007: \"Soft-delete/archive flag, not hard delete\". Confirm by the data-model decision for `notes`. |\n| `ASM-BE-603` | An unsupported Accept header returns 406 with the proposed error code `NOT_ACCEPTABLE`, not a fallback. | `backend.error-model` lists 406 for the spec endpoint. `GAP-BE-309` is open. |\n| `ASM-BE-604` | The endpoint serves JSON, because the path ends in `.json`. The content type is the JSON media type. | The path is itself proposed (`GAP-BE-303`). Confirm by `GAP-BE-819`. |\n| `ASM-BE-605` | The checks in `backend.api-docs.jobs` run on every CI build. | NFR-010 runs the scope-guardrail suite on every CI build. No input states this for the documentation checks (`GAP-BE-823`). |\n| `ASM-BE-606` | Coverage below 100% rejects the build (the build is not published). | NFR-008 targets 100% endpoint coverage and US-004-01 requires an endpoint to be documented no later than it is callable. US-004-04 only requires reporting. |\n\n**Open items from the project spec that touch this module**\n\n- `Q-BE-005` and `CON-BE-006` (email transport region and PII path).\n- `Q-BE-151` (the approved EU region list).\n- `GAP-BE-035` (field constraints).\n- `GAP-BE-089` (no tooling named).\n- `GAP-BE-303` (the path is proposed).\n- `GAP-BE-309` (Accept fallback).\n- `GAP-BE-500` (page size).\n- `GAP-BE-602` (the inbound handoffs: their content was not supplied to this module, so none could be read or answered).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-004-01", "US-004-02", "US-004-04", "US-004-05", "US-004-06", "US-005-01"]}, {"id": "backend/modules/scope-guardrail-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/scope-guardrail-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: scope-guardrail\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [CON-BE-007, GAP-BE-309]\nscope: []\n```\n`scope` is empty because this run supplied no operation records for this module. No input defines an endpoint that `scope-guardrail` owns, and no `API-` id is written here. The module acts through a request-rejection layer and a CI test suite (see `backend.scope-guardrail.requirements`). [US-005-02 to US-005-05; backend.module-map]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The `scope-guardrail` module keeps the API surface inside its documented boundary. Mobile-specific, attachment and real-time endpoints are excluded. [BR-014, BR-015, BR-016; backend.module-map] It owns four things:\n\n1. **Attachment rejection.** No attachment or file-upload route exists. A request to an attachment path, for example `POST /notes/{id}/attachments`, is rejected. [US-005-02; BR-014]\n2. **File-payload rejection.** A multipart/form-data request with a file payload, sent to a note create or update endpoint, is rejected with 415 and persists no file content. [US-005-02]\n3. **Real-time rejection.** No WebSocket or SSE channel exists. A WebSocket upgrade or SSE connection to any notes_api endpoint is rejected with no protocol upgrade. Collaboration has no live broadcast or merge logic. [US-005-03; BR-015]\n4. **Clear out-of-scope error.** A call to an unsupported mobile-specific, attachment or real-time endpoint returns a structured error. The error uses code `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and a human-readable message that references the scope exclusion. It is never a silent failure or a bare 404. [US-005-04; BR-016; NFR-011]\n5. **Contract test suite.** An automated suite runs on every CI build. It checks the published OpenAPI document and passes only when zero endpoints match an excluded category. A new endpoint matching an excluded pattern, for example `/attachments` or `/ws`, fails the suite and blocks the merge. [US-005-05; BR-014, BR-015; NFR-010]\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- The error envelope, status-code policy and error-code registry. They are owned by `backend.error-model`, and this module applies them.\n- The published OpenAPI document, its coverage metric and its Scope section, including the lint that fails a docs build when the Scope section is missing. These belong to `api-docs`. [US-005-01; backend.module-map]\n- Concurrent-PATCH semantics for a note. They belong to `notes`. This module only requires that no broadcast or merge logic exists (`BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-004`; `GAP-BE-309`).\n- Logging and classifying stakeholder requests for mobile, attachments or real-time collaboration. That is a product-management process outside backend. [BR-017; US-005-06; `GAP-BE-001`]\n- Authentication, roles and the permission matrix (`backend.auth-model`, `GAP-BE-085`).\n- Any UI.\n\n**Inbound handoffs.** HANDOFF-WEBAPP-163212, HANDOFF-WEBAPP-200601 and HANDOFF-WEBAPP-743710 are listed for this run. Their content was not supplied, so none could be read or answered here (`GAP-BE-602`).\n\n**Identifier note.** Ids minted by this module come from its reserved block: `ASM-BE-651` onward, `GAP-BE-867` onward, `INV-BE-260` onward. It mints no `CON-` and no `Q-`. All other ids are cited from the project spec.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.data-model | status: DERIVED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\n**No persisted entities.** This module owns no table, column, index or foreign key. No input asks it to store anything, and none of its stories carries a persistence requirement beyond \"no file content is persisted\" and \"no note is persisted\" on a rejected request. [US-005-02; US-005-04]\n\n- **Exclusion list.** The excluded categories are mobile-specific, attachment and real-time. [BR-014, BR-015, BR-016; US-005-01] They are held as static configuration in the codebase, read by both the request-rejection layer and the contract test suite, and are not stored in the database (`ASM-BE-651`). The path patterns inside each category are `ASM-BE-654` and `GAP-BE-867`.\n- **Rejected requests.** A rejected request writes nothing to PostgreSQL. This is required by `INV-BE-260`.\n- **PII.** The module stores no PII. Its error responses must not echo the request body or file content (`INV-BE-262`).\n- **Encryption and retention.** Not applicable, since nothing is stored.\n\n**Migrations.** None. This module is absent from `backend.migration-plan`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.state-machine | status: DERIVED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\n**No entity with a status.** The module holds no persisted state, so there are no states, no transitions and no terminal states to define. Each rejection decision is made per request and is stateless.\n\n**Request decision table.** This is not a state machine. The rows are evaluated in order, and the first match decides the response.\n\n| # | Condition | Result | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | WebSocket upgrade or SSE connection attempted on any notes_api endpoint | rejected; no protocol upgrade; status open (`GAP-BE-309`) | US-005-03; BR-015 |\n| 2 | path matches an attachment pattern (`ASM-BE-654`) | 404 with an `OUT_OF_SCOPE` body (`ASM-BE-653`, `CON-BE-007`) | US-005-02; US-005-04 |\n| 3 | path matches a mobile-specific pattern | rejected with `OUT_OF_SCOPE`; patterns undefined (`GAP-BE-867`) | BR-016 |\n| 4 | note create or update request with multipart/form-data content type (`ASM-BE-655`) | 415 with `OUT_OF_SCOPE`; no file content persisted | US-005-02; US-005-04 |\n| 5 | anything else | not handled by this module; falls through to normal routing (`GAP-BE-868`) | |\n\n**Contract-test outcome.** The suite result is `PASS` or `FAIL` per CI build and is not persisted by this module. `FAIL` blocks the merge. [US-005-05]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-001 Reject attachment paths\n**Given** the deployed API,\n**When** a request is sent to any attachment path, for example `POST /notes/{id}/attachments`,\n**Then** the response status is 404, the body is the error envelope from `backend.error-model` with code `OUT_OF_SCOPE`, and the message references the scope exclusion.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-02, US-005-04; BR-014, BR-016; NFR-011.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-attachment-path-404-out-of-scope` (planned).\n- **API.** none; this is a routing-layer response.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`. US-005-02 asks for 404, while US-005-04 and BR-016 ask for a message and not a bare 404. The reading used is `ASM-BE-653`. The contradiction is `CON-BE-007`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-002 Reject file payloads on note endpoints\n**Given** a note create or update endpoint,\n**When** a request with a multipart/form-data content type and a file payload is submitted,\n**Then** the response is 415 with code `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and a human-readable reason, no file content is persisted, and no note is created or changed.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-02, US-005-04; BR-014.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-multipart-415-no-persist` (planned).\n- **API.** none (the endpoints are owned by `notes`).\n- **Status.** Detection rule: `ASM-BE-655`. Which routes count as note endpoints: `GAP-BE-869`. Status 415 is shared with the login refusals, so consumers must switch on the error code (`backend.error-model`).\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-003 Reject real-time channels\n**Given** the running API server,\n**When** a client attempts a WebSocket upgrade or an SSE connection to any notes_api endpoint,\n**Then** the connection is rejected, no protocol upgrade occurs, and a standard error status is returned.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03; BR-015; BR-016.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-websocket-upgrade-rejected`, `test-scope-sse-rejected` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The exact status, and the body shape for a rejected upgrade, are `GAP-BE-309`. How an SSE attempt is recognised is `ASM-BE-654`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-004 No live broadcast or merge logic\n**Given** two clients submit PATCH requests to the same note concurrently,\n**When** both requests are processed,\n**Then** each is handled as an ordinary request, the API applies a standard last-write or conflict response, and no live broadcast or merge logic runs.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03; BR-015.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-concurrent-patch-no-broadcast` (planned).\n- **API.** none (the PATCH endpoint is owned by `notes`).\n- **Status.** Last-write versus conflict is `GAP-BE-309` and is decided in `notes`. This requirement fixes only the absence of broadcast and merge.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-005 Reject mobile-specific endpoints\n**Given** a call to an unsupported mobile-specific endpoint,\n**When** the request is made,\n**Then** the response carries code `OUT_OF_SCOPE` with a message referencing the scope exclusion, and not a silent failure or a bare 404.\n- **Traces to.** BR-016; US-005-01; US-005-04.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-mobile-path-out-of-scope` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** `BLOCKED` on `GAP-BE-867`: no input says what a mobile-specific path looks like.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-006 Documented, explanatory 4xx\n**Given** any call that this module rejects as out of scope,\n**When** the response is produced,\n**Then** it is a documented 4xx response carrying an explanatory message, in the shared error envelope.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-011; BR-016; US-005-04.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-all-rejections-use-envelope` (planned).\n- **API.** none. The response shape is defined once in `backend.error-model`, and `api-docs` documents it.\n- **Status.** The target is 100% of out-of-scope calls, per NFR-011. Error code names other than `OUT_OF_SCOPE` are proposals (`ASM-BE-301`).\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-007 Contract suite passes on a clean surface\n**Given** the CI pipeline runs,\n**When** the scope-guardrail suite executes against the OpenAPI document,\n**Then** it passes only if zero endpoints match the excluded categories (mobile, attachments, real-time).\n- **Traces to.** US-005-05; BR-014, BR-015; NFR-010.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-contract-clean-spec-passes` (planned).\n- **API.** none. The suite reads the document owned by `api-docs`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-008 Contract suite blocks an excluded endpoint\n**Given** a developer adds a new endpoint matching an excluded pattern, for example `/attachments` or `/ws`,\n**When** CI runs on the pull request,\n**Then** the guardrail test fails and the merge is blocked.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-05; NFR-010.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-contract-excluded-endpoint-fails` (planned, using a fixture spec containing `/attachments` and one containing `/ws`).\n- **API.** none.\n- **Status.** The patterns beyond the two examples are `ASM-BE-654` and `GAP-BE-867`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-009 Suite runs on every build\n**Given** any CI build,\n**When** the pipeline executes,\n**Then** the scope-guardrail suite runs, and the metric is 0 out-of-scope endpoints detected per build.\n- **Traces to.** NFR-010; US-005-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-scope-ci-runs-every-build` (planned, as a pipeline configuration check).\n- **API.** none.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\nNo HTTP operation is defined.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.invariants | status: BLOCKED -->\n## Invariants\n\n**BLOCKED.** the run did not produce this section for module `scope-guardrail`. Nothing has been written in its place.\n\nThis section is required by the surface's template and was not produced. It is emitted blocked rather than omitted: a missing section is invisible, a blocked one is a work item.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Authorization\n\n**No role invokes an operation of this module**, because the module owns no operation. It acts on requests addressed to other routes.\n\n- **Order of checks.** Out-of-scope matching runs before authentication, so that an integrator gets the documented error without needing a credential (`ASM-BE-652`). The `OUT_OF_SCOPE` response reveals only what the published Scope section already states. [US-005-01]\n- **Ownership predicate.** None. The module touches no user-scoped resource.\n- **Permissions.** Roles are undefined (`GAP-BE-085`, `ASM-BE-302`). The module needs no permission grant, since a rejection performs no privileged action.\n- **Disclosure.** A rejection says nothing about whether a note, user or session exists.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.jobs | status: DERIVED -->\n## 9. Jobs\n\n- **Runtime jobs.** None. There is no scheduled sweep, retry or background task in this module.\n- **CI job.** The contract suite runs on every CI build, and this is the only recurring work. [NFR-010; US-005-05] It runs on pull requests as well as on merged builds, because a failure must block the merge.\n- **Idempotency.** Not applicable. Rejections are stateless, and repeating a rejected request yields the same rejection.\n- **Retries.** None. A rejected request is not retried by the server.\n- **Tooling.** The CI system and the test framework are not named (`GAP-BE-089`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 10. Test plan\n\n**Unit.**\n- `test-scope-matcher-attachment-patterns`: the attachment pattern matcher, with positive and negative paths (`ASM-BE-654`).\n- `test-scope-multipart-detection`: content-type detection (`ASM-BE-655`), including case variants and a boundary parameter.\n- `test-scope-response-builder-fixed-text`: the response uses fixed message text and echoes nothing (`INV-BE-262`).\n\n**Integration.** Each runs against the application with a test PostgreSQL 16 database.\n- `test-scope-attachment-path-404-out-of-scope` (FR-001).\n- `test-scope-multipart-415-no-persist`: asserts row counts are unchanged after the request (FR-002, `INV-BE-260`).\n- `test-scope-websocket-upgrade-rejected` and `test-scope-sse-rejected` (FR-003).\n- `test-scope-concurrent-patch-no-broadcast` (FR-004).\n- `test-scope-all-rejections-use-envelope` (FR-006).\n- `test-scope-reject-before-auth`: an unauthenticated request to an excluded path returns `OUT_OF_SCOPE` and not 401 (`ASM-BE-652`).\n\n**Contract.** The suite itself, run against fixture specs.\n- `test-scope-contract-clean-spec-passes` (FR-007).\n- `test-scope-contract-excluded-endpoint-fails`, with fixtures containing `/attachments` and `/ws` (FR-008).\n- `test-scope-ci-runs-every-build` (FR-009).\n- `test-scope-contract-detects-real-time-in-spec`: a spec entry that declares a WebSocket or SSE channel fails the suite.\n\n**Blocked tests.** The mobile-specific case (FR-005) and the exact real-time status (FR-003) cannot be asserted until `GAP-BE-867` and `GAP-BE-309` are closed.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.gaps | status: DERIVED -->\n## 11. Gaps, contradictions and assumptions\n\nItems defined in the project spec are cited here, not copied.\n\n**Cited from the project spec.**\n- `CON-BE-007` (non-critical): US-005-02 requires 404 for attachment paths, while US-005-04 and BR-016 require a message and not a bare 404. This module follows `ASM-BE-653` and does not resolve it.\n- `GAP-BE-309`: the status for WebSocket and SSE rejection, and the concurrent-PATCH outcome, are open.\n- `GAP-BE-001`: US-005-06 belongs to no backend module.\n- `GAP-BE-602`: the three inbound handoffs were not supplied.\n- `GAP-BE-089`: no CI system, test framework or library is named.\n- `GAP-BE-085`, `ASM-BE-302`: authentication and roles.\n- `ASM-BE-301`: error code names other than `OUT_OF_SCOPE` are proposals.\n\n**Gaps raised by this module.**\n\n| ID | Gap | Degrades | Fallback | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-867` | No input defines what a mobile-specific endpoint or path is. | FR-005, FR-008 (partly), `INV-BE-261` | None. FR-005 is `BLOCKED`. | A list of excluded mobile path patterns |\n| `GAP-BE-868` | Behaviour for an unknown path outside every excluded category is not stated. | decision-table row 5 | Falls through to normal routing and the framework's default for unknown routes. | A statement of the unknown-route response |\n| `GAP-BE-869` | Which routes count as \"a note create/update endpoint\" for the multipart rule is not stated. US-005-02 names create/update only. | FR-002 | Applies to every note route that accepts a body, as named in `notes`. | The notes endpoint list, or a rule that covers all routes |\n| `GAP-BE-870` | No input says whether rejected out-of-scope calls are logged or counted in the API for the rejected-requests KPI. BR-017 assigns that count to a product-management log. | observability | Nothing is logged here beyond the standard request logging in `backend.observability`. | A decision on whether the API emits a metric for these rejections |\n\n**Assumptions made by this module.**\n- `ASM-BE-651`: the exclusion list is static configuration in the codebase, not a database table. No input says where it lives and none requires it to change at runtime. A different choice would only add a table.\n- `ASM-BE-652`: out-of-scope matching runs before authentication. BR-016 asks for a clear, documented error for integrators, and the Scope section is public documentation. Confirm with the owner of the authorisation model.\n- `ASM-BE-653`: an attachment-path request returns status 404 with an `OUT_OF_SCOPE` envelope. That satisfies US-005-02's status and US-005-04's message together. If the product owner prefers a different 4xx, `CON-BE-007` governs.\n- `ASM-BE-654`: an attachment pattern is any request path that has a segment equal to `attachments`. A real-time pattern is a path whose segment is `ws`, or a request carrying an `Upgrade: websocket` header or an `Accept: text/event-stream` header. US-005-05 names `/attachments` and `/ws` as examples and gives nothing further. Confirm with `GAP-BE-867`.\n- `ASM-BE-655`: a file payload is detected from a `Content-Type` of `multipart/form-data`, not from the body contents. US-005-02 and US-005-04 name multipart uploads as the case.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.gaps -->", "parent_ids": ["US-005-02", "US-005-03", "US-005-04", "US-005-05"]}, {"id": "backend/contracts/openapi.yaml", "type": "openapi", "path": "backend/contracts/openapi.yaml", "content": "openapi: 3.1.0\ninfo:\n  title: Backend API\n  version: 1.0.0\n  description: Operations the backend module specifications define, rendered from\n    their structured operation records. None was inferred.\npaths:\n  /auth/otp/send:\n    post:\n      operationId: API-AUTH-707260\n      summary: Request a 6-digit email one-time code for sign-in. Accepts at most\n        3 requests per email within a trailing 10 minutes (every request counts);\n        further requests return 415 and create no code and send no email. Not refused\n        during an account lock, but still subject to the resend limit. Response must\n        not reveal whether the email has a User.\n      description: Defined by module `auth` in `modules/auth-SPEC.md`.\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              required:\n              - email\n              properties:\n                email:\n                  type: string\n                  description: Email address to which the code is sent. Compared in\n                    normalised form (ASM-BE-015). Personal data; never logged unredacted.\n                    Format validation rule is unstated (GAP-BE-035).\n      responses:\n        '200':\n          description: Request a 6-digit email one-time code for sign-in. Accepts\n            at most 3 requests per email within a trailing 10 minutes (every request\n            counts); further requests return 415 and create no code and send no email.\n            Not refused during an account lock, but still subject to the resend limit.\n            Response must not reveal whether the email has a User.\n        '400':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-707260.\n        '415':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-707260.\n        '500':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-707260.\n        '503':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-707260.\n      x-devagent-source:\n        module: auth\n        document: modules/auth-SPEC.md\n        id: API-AUTH-707260\n  /auth/otp/verify:\n    post:\n      operationId: API-AUTH-692907\n      summary: Verify a 6-digit email one-time code. A correct, unexpired, unused\n        code on an unlocked account succeeds, consumes the code (single use, safe\n        under concurrent submission) and creates the User on first successful verification.\n        Locked account (15 minutes after 5 wrong codes), wrong code, expired code\n        (older than 10 minutes) and already-used code are each refused with 415, distinguished\n        only by the error code. Success status and credential returned are not fixed\n        by inputs (GAP-BE-604, Q-BE-012).\n      description: Defined by module `auth` in `modules/auth-SPEC.md`.\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              required:\n              - email\n              - code\n              properties:\n                email:\n                  type: string\n                  description: Email address the code was sent to. Compared in normalised\n                    form (ASM-BE-015). Personal data.\n                code:\n                  type: string\n                  description: The 6-digit numeric one-time code received by email\n                    (BR-029). Sent as a string so leading zeros survive.\n      responses:\n        '200':\n          description: Verify a 6-digit email one-time code. A correct, unexpired,\n            unused code on an unlocked account succeeds, consumes the code (single\n            use, safe under concurrent submission) and creates the User on first successful\n            verification. Locked account (15 minutes after 5 wrong codes), wrong code,\n            expired code (older than 10 minutes) and already-used code are each refused\n            with 415, distinguished only by the error code. Success status and credential\n            returned are not fixed by inputs (GAP-BE-604, Q-BE-012).\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                type: object\n                additionalProperties: false\n                required: []\n                properties:\n                  credential:\n                    type: string\n                    description: Credential granting authenticated access after successful\n                      verification. Form (body token, cookie or other) and lifetime\n                      are undecided (Q-BE-012, GAP-BE-302); may instead be delivered\n                      outside the body.\n        '400':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-692907.\n        '415':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-692907.\n        '500':\n          description: See `modules/auth-SPEC.md`, API-AUTH-692907.\n      x-devagent-source:\n        module: auth\n        document: modules/auth-SPEC.md\n        id: API-AUTH-692907\n  /notes:\n    post:\n      operationId: API-NOTES-232390\n      summary: Create a note with a mandatory non-empty title and body, optionally\n        pinned (US-002-01, BR-005, BR-018)\n      description: Defined by module `notes` in `modules/notes-SPEC.md`.\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              required:\n              - title\n              - body\n              properties:\n                title:\n                  type: string\n                  description: Note title; mandatory and non-empty at creation (BR-005)\n                body:\n                  type: string\n                  description: Note body; mandatory and non-empty at creation (BR-005)\n                pinned:\n                  type: boolean\n                  description: Whether the note is pinned; defaults to false (BR-018)\n      responses:\n        '201':\n          description: Create a note with a mandatory non-empty title and body, optionally\n            pinned (US-002-01, BR-005, BR-018)\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                type: object\n                additionalProperties: false\n                required:\n                - id\n                - title\n                - body\n                - status\n                - pinned\n                - tags\n                - created_at\n                - updated_at\n                properties:\n                  id:\n                    type: string\n                    description: Identifier of the created note\n                  title:\n                    type: string\n                    description: Note title\n                  body:\n                    type: string\n                    description: Note body\n                  status:\n                    type: string\n                    description: Note status, active or archived;
